Abstract: World action models jointly predict future visual observations and actions, whereas existing tactile-aware variants typically represent future touch as an image or latent stream without modeling the physical dependencies that organize tactile states hierarchically. We present HiTac-WAM, a hierarchical tactile world action model that forecasts a sequence of future tactile states for each candidate action chunk before execution. The forecast factorizes into contact state, a 3D deformation field, and slip risk, organized as a directed hierarchy in which each downstream stage is conditioned on stop-gradient signals from preceding stages. A directed attention mask allows tactile queries to attend to the video-action context of each candidate while preventing video and action queries from attending to tactile tokens. For planning, HiTac-WAM ranks candidate action chunks using tactile forecasts and task-progress estimates. For execution, the selected tactile forecast is retained as a reference; persistent discrepancies between predicted and observed tactile states trigger corrective replanning. HiTac-WAM achieves a mean contact F1 of 0.921; under matched training budgets, the directed hierarchy reduces 3D displacement L2 error by 17.6% relative to the deformation-only predictor and improves slip AUPRC by 60.4% relative to the slip-only predictor. Across chip grasping, blackboard erasing, and USB insertion, selection guided by the hierarchical forecasts increases the average real-robot success rate from 31.1% to 61.1%, while the full system attains 72.2%.

</details>


### [77] [OrthoSkillVLA: Continual Skill Learning via Gradient-Informed Skill Subspace Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19589)
*Jiaqi Wang,Zhou Fang,Qiongfeng Shi,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OrthoSkillVLA，一种面向预训练VLA模型的参数高效持续技能学习框架。通过分析VLM与ActionHead的不同表征角色，分别施加子空间约束，并引入轻量级特征感知MoE解码器来避免输出层冻结的瓶颈或更新的灾难性遗忘，在不回放演示的情况下保护旧技能并习得新技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的Vision-Language-Action (VLA)模型为机器人学习提供了强基础，但顺序适应多个技能会扰动先前技能的表示和速度映射，导致灾难性遗忘。现有方法中，基于架构的方法通过隔离技能改善保留但增加推理开销；基于子空间约束的方法虽然限制参数更新到正交子空间以减少干扰，但对整个模型施加统一约束，忽视了VLA组件各自的角色差异。

Method: OrthoSkillVLA框架：(1) 对VLM和ActionHead分别施加独立的子空间约束——VLM负责广泛语义表征，约束旨在保留可复用语义容量；ActionHead将语义细化为局部速度模式，约束旨在保护这些敏感的速度映射。(2) 提出轻量级特征感知MoE解码器替代直接冻结或整体更新的输出层：每个技能分配一个紧凑专家，训练无关的路由器根据特征空间亲和度选择专家，避免输出层表达力瓶颈同时防止覆盖先前速度映射。

Result: 在模拟环境和真实世界的大量评估以及消融实验中，OrthoSkillVLA在获取新技能的同时更好地保留了先前技能，优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过识别VLA内部组件的异质性表征角色并差异化处理，以及采用特征感知MoE解码器解决输出层困境，OrthoSkillVLA为预训练VLA模型的持续技能学习提供了无需演示回放的有效方案。

Abstract: Pretrained Vision-Language-Action models provide a strong foundation for robot learning, but sequentially adapting them to diverse skills can perturb the representations and velocity mappings used by previous skills, leading to catastrophic forgetting. Architecture-based approaches improve retention by isolating skills but lead to increased inference footprint. Recent subspace-constrained methods restrict parameter updates in an orthogonal subspace to minimize interference but impose a unified constraint on the entire model. We analyze the distinct roles of internal VLA components and identify two VLA-specific challenges. First, the VLM maintains broad semantic representations, making it vulnerable to capacity exhaustion, whereas the ActionHead refines semantics into localized velocity patterns that are highly sensitive to perturbations. Second, the final velocity decoder serves as a readout layer. Freezing it forms an output-stage expressivity bottleneck, while updating it risks overwriting previous velocity mappings. To this end, we propose OrthoSkillVLA, a parameter-efficient framework for continual skill learning in pretrained VLA models without demonstration replay. Given the representation heterogeneity, we impose separate subspace constraints on the VLM and ActionHead, preserving reusable semantic capacity while protecting localized velocity patterns. For the output layer, we introduce a lightweight feature-aware MoE decoder, where each skill is allocated a compact expert and a training-free router selects the expert according to feature-space affinity. Extensive simulated and real-world evaluations, together with ablations, demonstrate that OrthoSkillVLA better preserves prior skills while acquiring new ones.

</details>


### [78] [The Verification Gap in Networked Physical AI: A Post-Semantic Communication Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19593)
*Shunsuke Saruwatari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出后语义通信框架（Post-Semantic Communication Framework），用于解决网络化物理AI中"提案已验证但与物理执行之间存在证据/授权缺口"的验证鸿沟问题。框架区分证据转移与证据协调，并通过有限状态机检查和受控通信研究验证了最终化方依赖的不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务有效的提案并不等同于合理的物理动作。在网络化物理AI中，即使提案被理解且有效，但提出时缺乏有效证据或执行所需的授权，动作仍无法最终化。这种"验证鸿沟"（verification gap）是现有通信框架未系统处理的系统接口问题。

Method: 提出后语义通信框架，包含四个核心要素：(1) 应用声明的证据需求；(2) 将合格观测表示为证据记录；(3) 通过单一路径验证支持性与冲突性记录；(4) 将证据充分性与授权最终化及其下游运行时门分离。框架进一步区分证据转移（扩大最终化者可达的记录集）与证据协调（抑制最终化端已有记录的传输），并通过有限状态机框架检查验证评估器实现的一致性。

Result: 受控通信研究揭示了最终化方依赖的不对称性：发送方最终化的Feedback模式利用证据转移在可行绘图区域扩展证据可达性；而接收方最终化的Feedback模式则利用证据协调抑制冗余负载，直到丢失率、延迟、新鲜度和截止时间等成本促使选择转向One-way模式。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一个可验证的框架来弥合提案形成与物理执行之间的验证鸿沟，并通过情景级报告模式定义了未来物理AI测量研究的共同基准，为物理AI通信系统的系统化设计与评估奠定了基础。

Abstract: A task-effective proposal is not yet a justified physical action. In networked Physical AI, a proposal may be understood while valid, timely, proposal-bound evidence or the authority required to finalize an action remains unavailable. We call this mismatch the verification gap and introduce a Post-Semantic Communication Framework for the systems interface between proposal formation and physical execution. The framework begins with application-declared evidence requirements, represents qualifying observations as evidence records, validates supporting and conflicting records through one path, and separates evidence sufficiency from authorized finalization and a downstream runtime gate. It further distinguishes evidence transfer, which can enlarge the record set reachable by a finalizer, from evidence coordination, which can suppress transmission around records already held at the finalization endpoint. Finite-state framework checks verify that the evaluator implements the declared distinctions consistently. Under the declared model, the controlled communication study exposes a finalizer-dependent asymmetry: sender-finalized Feedback uses evidence transfer to expand evidence reachability throughout the feasible plotted region, whereas receiver-finalized Feedback uses coordination to suppress redundant payload until loss, latency, freshness, and deadline costs shift selection to One-way. Finally, an episode-level reporting schema defines common denominators for future measured Physical-AI studies.

</details>


### [79] [What Matters for Latent Actions in Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19613)
*Xizhou Bu,Qingda Hu,Lei Zhou,Lingfeng Zhang,Yingbo Tang,Zihao Liu,Xinyi Tao,Zhiqiang Ma,Qingqiu Huang,Chufeng Tang,Hongbo Wang,Jing Zhang,Jiayi Ma,Hangjun Ye,Wei Li,Xiaoshuai Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是首个针对潜动作学习（LAM）用于机器人操控的全面实证研究，在统一的自编码框架下，系统研究了41种LAM设计选择（涵盖三大维度），评估了4种代理指标，并在三个广泛使用的基准及真实世界机器人任务上验证了微调VLM骨干网络结合潜动作能为下游策略学习提供更强的初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LAM已成为机器人利用大规模无标签视频学习的重要范式，但该领域研究高度碎片化，现有方法在不同实验设置下孤立地评估各设计选择，缺乏统一的比较框架，难以识别真正决定下游机器人操控性能的关键因素。

Method: 将代表性LAM方法统一到公共的自编码框架中，系统调查41种LAM设计选择，涵盖三个维度：（1）潜动作建模范式、（2）学习目标与正则化方法、（3）潜动作集成策略。同时考察四种代理指标以评估其预测下游机器人操控性能的可靠性。在三个基准数据集上开展广泛实验，并在真实世界机器人操控任务上进一步验证。

Result: 实验提供了强有力的经验证据，表明使用潜动作微调视觉语言模型（VLM）骨干网络相比其他方案能为下游策略学习提供更强的初始化，这一结论在三个基准数据集和真实世界机器人操控任务上均得到验证。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了LAM领域缺乏统一实证比较的空白，明确了关键设计选择对下游操控性能的影响，为未来LAM方法的设计和评估提供了系统性的参考依据。

Abstract: Latent Action Models (LAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for enabling robot learning to leverage large-scale unlabeled videos through latent actions that serve as compact surrogates for physical actions. Despite rapid progress, research on LAM remains highly fragmented, with existing methods evaluating different design choices in isolation under inconsistent experimental settings, making it difficult to identify the factors that truly determine downstream robotic manipulation performance. In this work, we present the first comprehensive empirical study of latent action learning for robotic manipulation. We unify representative LAM methods within a common autoencoding framework and systematically investigate 41 LAM design choices across three dimensions, including latent action modeling paradigms, learning objectives and regularization methods, and latent action integration strategies. We further examine four proxy metrics for evaluating latent action quality and assess their ability to reliably predict downstream robotic manipulation performance. Extensive experiments on three widely used benchmarks provide strong empirical evidence that fine-tuning vision-language model (VLM) backbones with latent actions provides a stronger initialization for downstream policy learning, with further validation on real-world robot manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [80] [Magnetically Self-Sealed MR Haptic Actuator With PWM-Based Excitation and High-Fidelity Torque Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19635)
*Dong Qiang,Tian Yuan,Song Yang,Kequan Xia,Thomas Reddyhoff,Yikun Zhang,Cheng Cheng,Min Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成式磁流变液(MRF)触觉系统，包含紧凑型磁自密封旋转执行器、低迟滞PWM驱动、基于模型的高保真扭矩渲染，以及长时间运行的稳定性能。实验表明，10kHz PWM频率下迟滞显著降低，结合前馈、迟滞补偿、PI反馈和滑模校正的实时控制器相比PID在方波过冲、欠冲和稳态RMSE上分别降低77.4%、61.9%和68.3%，且1.5小时运行后线圈温度仅上升2.5°C，无跟踪性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRF执行器作为触觉反馈方案具有紧凑和快速可控的优势，但实际应用中面临可靠密封、低迟滞激励、精确扭矩控制和长期稳定运行等挑战。现有方案难以同时满足这些需求，需要一种集成的MRF触觉系统来解决上述问题。

Method: 通过磁静力学仿真指导磁性和非磁性材料排布以聚焦磁通于多盘扭矩和永磁密封区域，实现最大600 N·mm/A输出。采用高PWM频率(10kHz)降低迟滞并提高重复性，建立随扭矩变化方向和速度变化的非线性模型。控制器结合前馈、迟滞补偿、PI反馈和滑模修正实现高保真扭矩控制。

Result: 相比PID控制器，所提出的控制器在方波过冲、欠冲和稳态RMSE上分别降低77.4%、61.9%和68.3%。系统能够跟踪正弦和生物力学模型参考信号，且在1.5小时长时测试中线圈温度仅上升2.5°C，无明显跟踪丢失。

Conclusion: 该高保真扭矩渲染技术将从根本上改变人机协作方式，使交互更加安全、高效和直观，推动了MRF执行器在触觉领域走向实际应用。

Abstract: Accurate and stable torque rendering is essential for safe and perceptive human--machine interaction. Magnetorheological fluid (MRF)-based actuators offer a compact and rapidly controllable solution for haptic feedback, but their practical implementation requires reliable fluid sealing, low-hysteresis excitation, accurate torque control, and stable long-duration operation. This article presents an integrated MRF haptic system featuring a compact magnetically self-sealed rotary actuator, low-hysteresis PWM operation, high-fidelity model-based torque rendering, and stable performance during long-time operation. Magnetostatic simulation guides the arrangement of magnetic and nonmagnetic materials to focus flux in the multidisk torque and permanent-magnet sealing regions, enabling a maximum 600 N$\cdot$mm/A output. Experiments show that higher PWM frequencies reduce hysteresis and improve repeatability. At 10 kHz, the response is represented by a nonlinear model that varies with the direction and speed of torque change. The real-time controller combines feedforward, hysteresis compensation, PI feedback, and sliding-mode correction. Compared with PID, it reduces square-wave overshoot, undershoot, and steady-state RMSE by 77.4\%, 61.9\%, and 68.3\%, respectively. It tracks sinusoidal and biomechanics-model-based references, and a 1.5-h test shows only a 2.5 $^\circ$C rise near the coil with no clear tracking loss. This high-fidelity torque rendering will fundamentally transform human--robot collaboration by making interactions safer, more efficient, and more intuitive.

</details>


### [81] [World-Model-Grounded LLM Planning for AUV and ASV Navigation Near Offshore Wind Farms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19661)
*Markus Buchholz,Ignacio Carlucho,Yvan R. Petillot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合世界模型的语言模型（LLM）规划器方案，通过物理驱动的神经世界模型、三段式基于梯度的轨迹优化器以及带信任域保护的模型预测控制（MPC）闭环重规划器，赋予LLM物理感知能力。该方法在6自由度AUV和3自由度ASV两类海洋机器人上评估，实现零预测碰撞，并相比无物理基线的GazeboSim目标距离误差降低70-93%。此外，对ASV展示了基于VLM的语义映射流水线，可从卫星图像、海图和API提取障碍物信息，达到96%可导航性精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能将自然语言任务转化为机器人动作序列，但缺乏物理直觉——无法判断命令应执行多长时间，也无法预见动作是否会导致机器人漂移撞上障碍物。因此需要引入世界模型来弥补LLM在物理常识上的缺失。

Method: 该方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 物理驱动的神经世界模型，用于预测机器人状态演化；(2) 三段式基于梯度的轨迹优化器，将LLM的语义高层意图转化为具体的执行时长与轨迹；(3) MPC风格的闭环重规划器，配备信任域保护机制，保证重规划的安全性和稳定性。此外，ASV平台还集成了VLM辅助的语义映射流水线，从卫星影像、海图和预测API中提取障碍物与环境上下文，替代机载传感器。

Result: 在五个基准任务中，6-DOF AUV和3-DOF ASV均达到所有目标且零预测碰撞。在GazeboSim中考虑洋流、波浪和推进器动力学后，经过残差微调（将替代翻滚均方根误差降低60%-69%），目标距离误差相比无物理基线减少：ASV降低70-82%，AUV降低约93%。ASV的VLM语义映射流水线达到96%可导航性精度，可无缝替代手工指定障碍物几何。

Conclusion: 世界模型能有效扩展LLM规划器的物理感知能力，使语言模型负责决策"做什么"、世界模型负责判断"做多久"，二者协同可在真实仿真环境（含洋流、波浪等扰动）中实现高可靠的无碰撞导航。VLM语义映射进一步证明了多模态大模型在环境感知层面的补充价值。

Abstract: Large language models can turn a natural-language mission into a sequence of robot actions, but they do not have a sense of physics: they cannot judge how long a command should run, or whether it will make the robot drift into an obstacle. We proposed the use of a world model to expand the capabilities of Large Language model-based planners. Our method has three components: a physics-grounded neural world model, a three-phase gradient-based trajectory optimizer, and a Model Predictive Controller (MPC)-style closed-loop replanner with a trust-region guard. The language model decides what to do, and the world model decides how long, whether that means driving eight thrusters through 6 DOF or two differential thrusters through 3 DOF. We evaluate two marine vehicle classes operating near offshore wind infrastructure: a 6-DOF Autonomous Underwater Vehicle (AUV) and a 3-DOF differential-drive Autonomous Surface Vehicle (ASV). In five benchmark missions per platform, both vehicles reach every goal with zero predicted collisions, and both transfer to GazeboSim under ocean current, waves, and thruster dynamics, remaining collision-free and cutting GazeboSim goal-distance error versus the ungrounded baseline by 70-82% (ASV) and roughly 93% (AUV), after a residual fine-tuning pass that separately reduces surrogate rollout Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) by 60% (AUV) and 69% (ASV). For the ASV we further demonstrate a Vision language model (VLM)-assisted semantic-mapping pipeline that extracts obstacles and environmental context from satellite imagery, nautical charts, and forecast Application Programming Interface (API) instead of onboard sensors, reaching 96% navigability accuracy as a drop-in replacement for hand-specified obstacle geometry.

</details>


### [82] [SAGE: Ergodic Control for Autonomous and Adaptive Inspection of Subsea Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19671)
*Markus Buchholz,Ignacio Carlucho,Yvan R. Petillot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE（Semantic and Adaptive Generative Ergodicity），一种基于遍历控制的自主水下巡检架构，用于海底采油树（XTs）阀门的按需检查。与固定A*路线不同，SAGE根据实时传感器推导的风险分布动态分配巡检时间，使车辆时间与风险比例相匹配。在五阀门双XT仿真场景中，SAGE对高危阀门的重访间隔为5.8秒，优于A*固定的8.1秒，且无需显式重规划或人工介入即可响应新发现的泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海底采油树阀门需要持续巡检，但并非所有阀门风险相同，且风险会随任务进展动态变化。固定路线无法按风险优先级分配巡检资源，也不能实时响应新发现的泄漏，因此需要一种能自适应风险分布的巡检控制架构。

Method: 提出SAGE（Semantic and Adaptive Generative Ergodicity）遍历控制架构。其核心思想是让车辆时间的空间分布与实时的、由传感器推导的风险分布成正比（遍历性）。风险分布每控制周期重新计算而非预先规划一次，新泄漏检测会自然影响下一周期的车辆行为。研究在五阀门双采油树场景中对比固定循环A*路线与SAGE。

Result: 在仿真中，SAGE对主导风险阀门的重访间隔为5.8秒，而A*对所有阀门固定为8.1秒（无论风险如何），意味着泄漏未被发现的时间可缩短至原来的约三分之二。两种方法可调谐使总时间相近，但只有遍历控制在重复检查高频方面表现更优。新检测到的泄漏无需显式重规划或操作员介入即可在下一控制周期改变车辆行为。

Conclusion: 遍历控制相比固定路线巡检在动态风险环境下的按需检查中具有显著优势：既能按风险比例分配时间，又能高频重访高风险目标，且天然具备自适应能力，无需离散的重新路由步骤。

Abstract: Subsea Christmas Trees (XTs) are underwater structures that use valves for directing oil flow, needing constant inspection. But not every valve carries the same risk at the same time: a valve with a suspected leak needs to be revisited far more often than one with a clean history, and that risk picture changes during the mission as new leaks are found. To handle this, we present SAGE (Semantic and Adaptive Generative Ergodicity), an ergodic-control architecture that allocates vehicle time in proportion to a live, sensor-derived risk distribution rather than a scripted route. We study a two-XT scenario, with five valves in total, and compare a fixed-loop A* tour against SAGE. Both methods can be tuned to spend similar total time near a high-risk valve, but only ergodic control also checks it more often: in simulation, a dominant-risk valve was revisited every 5.8 s under ergodic control against a fixed 8.1 s for every valve under A*, regardless of risk, so a leak can go unnoticed for barely two-thirds as long. Because the tracked distribution is recomputed rather than planned once, a newly detected leak shifts vehicle behavior on the next control cycle with no explicit re-planning step and no operator in the loop, which a fixed tour cannot do without a discrete re-route. We derive the ergodic control law behind this behavior and report simulation results on the five-valve scenario.

</details>


### [83] [Keeping the Franka Emika Panda alive: a ROS 2 stack with a reliable position interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19740)
*Antonio Langella,Davide Risi,Vincenzo Petrone,Enrico Ferrentino,Pasquale Chiacchio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开源软件栈，恢复了 Franka Emika Panda 机器人的 ROS 2 支持，并解决了其外部位置控制接口长期存在的不稳定问题。作者分析了不稳定的根本原因（外部控制环时序与采样抖动），并引入了异步硬件接口、速率匹配机制和位置域参考生成策略，实验验证了其在多种应用场景下的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Franka Emika Panda 机器人的外部位置控制接口长期存在不稳定问题（振动和保护性停机），且官方实现缺乏可靠的 ROS 2 支持。作者希望找到根本原因并提供一个现代、可靠、开源的 ROS 2 生态系统，以降低人机协作应用开发的准入门槛。

Method: 1) 分析不稳定的根本原因，证明振动和停机源于外部控制环的时序和采样抖动而非机器人自身限制；2) 提出异步硬件接口，将实时通信与 ROS 2 控制环解耦；3) 引入速率匹配机制以适配较慢的命令源；4) 采用位置域参考生成策略，生成可靠平滑的位置命令。

Result: 实验表明该架构能可靠跟踪速度参考，减少官方实现引入的运动伪影；该软件栈在运动规划、柔顺控制、位置控制操作和触觉遥操作等多类应用中于两个独立 Panda 平台上得到验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为 Panda 恢复了一个现代、可靠且开源的 ROS 2 生态，降低了开发安全、响应及时且可复现的人机协作应用（集成规划、感知、交互与共享自主）的门槛。

Abstract: This paper presents an open-source software stack that restores ROS 2 support for the Franka Emika Panda robot while resolving the long-standing unreliability of its external position control interface. We first analyze the root causes of unstable position control and show that the observed vibrations and protective stops arise from the timing of the external control loop and sampling jitter, rather than from limitations of the robot itself. Building on this analysis, we introduce an asynchronous hardware interface that decouples real-time communication from the ROS 2 control loop, a rate-matching mechanism for slower command sources, and a position-domain reference generation strategy that produces reliable, smooth position commands. Experimental validation shows that the proposed architecture reliably tracks velocity references by reducing motion artifacts introduced by the official implementation, and the stack is validated across motion planning, compliance control, position-controlled manipulation, and haptic teleoperation on two independent Panda platforms. By restoring a modern, reliable, and open ROS 2 ecosystem for the Panda, this work lowers the barrier to developing safe, responsive, and reproducible human-robot collaboration applications that integrate planning, perception, interaction, and shared autonomy. Code and videos are available on our website at https://sites.google.com/view/fer-ros2/.

</details>


### [84] [GOAG: Generative and Object-Agnostic Grasp Planner for Dexterous Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19759)
*Julien Merand,Boris Meden,Mathieu Grossard,Liming Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GOAG，一种面向灵巧机器人抓取的生成式、与物体无关的抓取规划器。其核心洞察是抓取器与物体在接触点处具有相同的表面几何形状，因此模型仅学习抓取器接触表面的压缩潜表征，推理时才引入物体特征，从而在无需针对特定物体训练数据的情况下高效采样有效抓取配置。在MultiDex数据集上取得86.93%的平均成功率，达到SOTA水平，且生成速度显著更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指抓取是关键的机器人技能，但当前基于深度学习的抓取规划器受限于在有限的、物体特定的数据集上训练，难以泛化到新物体。现有方法依赖物体特定的训练数据，无法有效应对未见过的物体，亟需一种与物体无关的抓取规划方法以解决泛化问题。

Method: GOAG基于抓取器与物体在接触点共享相同表面几何这一观察，构建一个深度生成模型，学习特定抓取器接触表面分布的紧凑潜表征，从而无需物体特定训练数据即可高效采样有效抓取配置。物体特征仅在推理阶段引入，用于检索与抓取器能力兼容的可行接触区域。

Result: 在MultiDex数据集上平均成功率达86.93%，达到最先进水平；生成大量抓取时处理速度显著更快，性能与针对该数据集专门训练的主流方法相当，且不依赖物体特定训练数据。

Conclusion: GOAG通过物体无关的生成式学习有效解决了传统数据驱动抓取规划器的泛化挑战，在模拟和真实场景中对多种不同抓取器均验证了有效性，展示了物体无关学习的优势。

Abstract: Multifingered grasping is a crucial robotic skill, but current deep-learning grasp planners often struggle to generalize to new objects because they are trained on limited, object-specific datasets. We introduce a fundamentally different approach, grounded in the observation that the gripper and the object share identical surface geometry at their mutual contact points. We propose GOAG: Generative and Object-Agnostic Grasp Planner for Dexterous Robotic Manipulation, a novel deep generative model that learns a compact latent representation of a specific gripper's contact surface distribution, enabling the efficient sampling of valid grasp configurations without relying on object-specific training data. We show that by introducing object features only at inference time, our model can effectively retrieve admissible contact areas that are compatible with the gripper's capabilities. We validate our approach through extensive experiments on established grasp protocols in both simulated and real-world scenarios, demonstrating its effectiveness with different grippers from the literature. Our method delivers state-of-the-art results on the objects from the MultiDex dataset, achieving an average success rate of 86.93%. It offers significantly faster processing when generating numerous grasps, while matching the performance of leading approaches specifically trained on this dataset. Unlike these methods, our approach does not rely on object-specific training data, highlighting the advantages of object-agnostic learning. It effectively addresses the generalization challenges faced by traditional data-driven grasp planners. Code and videos are available on our project website https://cea-list.github.io/goagweb/ .

</details>


### [85] [CoToGrasp: Contact-Topology-Conditioned Dexterous Grasp Synthesis via Canonical Workspace Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19776)
*Julien Merand,Boris Meden,Liming Chen,Mathieu Grossard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoToGrasp是一个完全在物体无关（object-agnostic）方式下训练的生成式框架，能够在无需昂贵物体标注数据集的情况下，合成严格限定于特定接触拓扑的多样化、稳定的灵巧抓取姿态，并在未见物体上实现零样本泛化，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的灵巧抓取规划器主要优化物理稳定性，只关注物体能否被抓取，而不关心应如何抓取以支持下游功能任务。虽然可以基于人类抓取分类法（taxonomy）来约束抓取合成，但需要代价高昂的物体标注数据集，构成严重的瓶颈。

Method: 提出CoToGrasp生成框架：1) 引入基于特征的规范工作空间（canonical workspace），将局部物体特征投影到统一的抓取器中心域，从而将语义功能意图与任意物体几何解耦；2) 在该工作空间内学习抓取器的内在接触流形（contact manifold）；3) 整个训练过程完全在物体无关方式下进行，实现零样本泛化。

Result: 在大型DexGraspNet数据集上的大量评估表明，CoToGrasp取得了SOTA性能，超越现有taxonomy导向的规划器；并在物理机器人平台上验证了合成接触拓扑的物理可行性与运动学可行性。

Conclusion: 通过特征化规范工作空间实现语义意图与物体几何解耦，CoToGrasp在无需数据收集瓶颈的情况下实现了对特定接触拓扑的严格条件化抓取合成，并具备良好的泛化能力与物理可行性。

Abstract: Current dexterous grasp planners primarily optimize for physical stability, focusing on whether an object can be grasped rather than how it should be grasped to support downstream functional tasks. However, conditioning grasp synthesis on specific human grasp taxonomies typically requires prohibitively expensive, object-annotated datasets. To address these limitations, we propose CoToGrasp, a novel generative framework that synthesizes diverse, stable grasps strictly conditioned on specific contact topologies. To bypass the data collection bottleneck, CoToGrasp is trained entirely in an object-agnostic manner. We introduce a feature-based canonical workspace that projects local object features into a unified gripper-centric domain, effectively decoupling the semantic functional intent from the arbitrary object geometry. By learning the intrinsic contact manifold of the gripper within this workspace, our model achieves zero-shot generalization to unseen objects at inference. Extensive evaluations on the large-scale DexGraspNet dataset demonstrate that CoToGrasp achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming existing taxonomy-guided planners. Finally, we demonstrate the physical viability and kinematic feasibility of our synthesized contact topologies on a physical robot platform. Code is available on our project website https://cea-list.github.io/cotograspweb/ .

</details>


### [86] [MILD: Tractable Terrain Modeling for Learning Improved Bipedal Locomotion on Deformable Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19955)
*Zeren Luo,Jiahui Zhang,Zhe Xu,Wanyue Li,Xinqi Li,Xuechao Chen,Zhangguo Yu,Annan Tang,Peng Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MILD系统，通过物理驱动的离散元接触求解器精确模拟空间变化的脚-地形交互，并利用深度强化学习训练地形感知控制器，使双足机器人在可变形（柔软）地形上实现自然适应和在线地形识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双足机器人在灾难响应和行星探索等应用中极具潜力，但其在可变形地面上的运动能力受到限制。现有仿真器无法捕捉可屈服基底的时空异质性，导致训练出的控制器在真实柔软表面上表现不佳。

Method: 1) 提出MILD系统，包含基于物理的离散元接触求解器，用于准确模拟空间变化的脚-地形交互；2) 通过深度强化学习训练地形感知控制器，采用潜在调制和本体感估计机制，使控制器能够感知并适应地形变化。

Result: 与最先进方法相比，MILD在训练过程中生成了更多样、更真实的接触场景，产生的控制器在真实可变形表面上表现出自然的适应性。硬件实验验证了该系统具备跨广泛表面刚度的在线地形识别与适应能力。

Conclusion: MILD通过物理精确的仿真模型结合地形感知的强化学习框架，有效解决了双足机器人在可变形地形上行走的难题，为实际应用中跨表面刚度的自适应控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Enabling robots to walk on yielding terrain is vital for applications ranging from disaster response to planetary exploration. While bipedal robots hold immense potential, their locomotion on deformable surfaces remains limited as current simulators fail to capture the spatiotemporal heterogeneity of such yielding substrates. We present MILD, featuring a physics-grounded discrete-element contact solver that accurately simulates spatially varying foot-terrain interactions. Complementing this model, we train a terrain-aware locomotion controller via deep reinforcement learning with latent modulation and proprioceptive estimation. Quantitative comparisons against state-of-the-art methods show our approach generates more diverse and realistic contact scenarios during training, resulting in controllers that exhibit natural adaptation on real deformable surfaces. Through hardware experiments, we demonstrate the system's capability for online terrain identification and adaptation across a wide range of surface stiffness.

</details>


### [87] [PVRA: A Pointwise Key-point Voting Framework for Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19968)
*Kulunu Samarawickrama,Roel Pieters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过比较分析相关领域研究，提出三维关键点模块化学习框架以学习装配依赖关系，从RGB-D输入中推断可操作输出，并在装配位姿估计数据集上训练和评估，相比物体中心基线方法在渐进装配指标上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代计算机视觉已实现机器人装配操作的部分自主性，但执行渐进式装配的自主操作需要比物体感知更专门的能力。作者通过比较分析指出，物体中心感知必须向学习装配依赖关系方向推进，以预测有意义且可操作的输出，从而实现自主装配操作。

Method: 提出一个基于3D关键点的模块化学习框架，从装配场景的RGB-D输入中学习装配依赖关系，推断可操作的输出（如装配位姿）。框架在装配位姿估计数据集上进行训练，并采用针对渐进装配增强的指标集与物体中心基线方法进行对比评估。

Result: 训练的网络在装配位姿估计数据集上进行了评估，与物体中心基线方法相比，在针对渐进装配设计的增强指标集上取得了更优的性能。

Conclusion: 基于3D关键点的模块化学习框架能够有效学习装配依赖关系，比传统的物体中心感知方法更适合渐进式装配的自主操作任务，为自主装配操作提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Modern computer vision has enabled partial autonomy in robotic assembly manipulation. However, performing autonomous manipulation of a progressive assembly demands a more specific set of skills, in addition to perceiving the objects. Through a comparative analysis of research in the associated domains, we deduce that object-centric perception must advance towards learning assembly dependencies to predict meaningful actionable outputs for autonomous assembly manipulation. Subsequently, we present a 3D keypoint-based modular learning framework to learn assembly dependencies to infer actionable outputs given a RGB-D input of an assembly scene. We train and evaluate our trained network on an assembly pose estimation dataset and compare it against object-centric baselines with an augmented set of metrics for progressive assemblies.

</details>


### [88] [Learning Highly Dynamic Skills Transition for Quadruped Jumping Through Constrained Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19977)
*Zeren Luo,Jiahui Zhang,Yimin Han,Ji Ma,Minghao Lu,Ioannis Havoutis,Peng Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层强化学习流水线，使四足机器人能够像真实动物一样通过狭窄障碍物（窄门）执行爆发性、侵略性的运动行为。通过模仿学习训练底层策略模仿动物动作，高层控制器结合视觉检测的门信息与底层技能能力，动态规划无碰撞轨迹穿越狭门，并可扩展到其他高动态任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有腿动物能够在受限空间中执行爆发性运动，但在四足机器人上复现这一行为长期以来是一个挑战。现有方法难以让四足机器人具备类动物的敏捷性，尤其是在穿越狭窄障碍物等高度动态场景中。

Method: 采用分层强化学习框架：底层使用模仿学习训练策略，模仿真实动物的行为并形成多样化技能库；高层控制器通过视觉检测获取门的信息，感知底层技能的能力边界，选择合适策略以生成无碰撞轨迹动态穿越障碍物。

Result: 该框架能够使四足机器人自主、敏捷地完成穿越空中窄门的任务，并且该框架可扩展到其他高度动态任务中。这项工作属于首批在地面行走机器人上实现自主空中门穿越任务的成果之一。

Conclusion: 该分层强化学习流水线成功赋予四足机器人接近生物对应物的敏捷性，证明了从动物行为中提取技能并用于机器人自主导航的可行性，为仿生四足机器人实现激进运动提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Although legged animals are capable of performing explosive motions while traversing confined spaces, replicating this behavior in quadrupedal robots has been a longstanding challenge. Here, we propose a hierarchical reinforcement learning pipeline that empowers the robots to perform aggressive locomotion through constrained obstacles--a narrow gate. The imitation learning technique is used to train the low-level policy, which mimics the behaviors of real animals and forms a set of diverse skills. The high-level controller, having an awareness of the capability of low-level skills and acquiring the gate information via vision-based detection, determines the suitable maneuvers with collision-free trajectories to traverse it dynamically. Notably, we also verify that this framework can be extended to other highly dynamic tasks. This is one of the first works that perform autonomous and agile aerial gate traversal tasks on ground-walking robots, extending the lifelike agility of legged robots to match that of their biological counterparts.

</details>


### [89] [Evidence-Gated Task and Motion Planning with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20084)
*Tsunehiko Tanaka,Matthew Stephenson,Alistair Macvicar,Edgar Simo-Serra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EAFG框架通过VLM生成探索性子目标并结合TAMP执行，在部分可观测环境下获取视觉证据，利用可行性门控决定继续任务规划、获取更多证据或停止，从而解决长时程操作任务中因对象不确定性导致的执行失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境中，机器人执行自然语言指令驱动的长时程操作任务时，VLMs与TAMP结合的方案可能仅依赖VLM的先验知识生成子目标，缺乏观测支持，导致执行失败或产生非预期结果。作者旨在解决这一不确定性下目标对象可用性难以判断的问题。

Method: 提出EAFG框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 通过VLM生成探索性子目标，结合TAMP执行以获取视觉证据；(2) 应用可行性门控（feasibility gate）决策下一步动作——继续任务规划、获取更多证据或停止执行。框架在规划前先通过探索发现任务相关对象，避免依赖无观测支撑的先验假设。

Result: 在对象使用存在歧义的烹饪任务中，EAFG通过在执行前发现任务相关对象，提高了食谱完成率；对于要求缺失对象的指令，EAFG促使系统做出恰当的停止决策，并减少对缺失对象的重复操作尝试。

Conclusion: EAFG有效融合了VLM的语义推理能力与TAMP的几何规划能力，通过主动证据获取与可行性门控机制，兼顾了部分可观测环境下任务规划的正确性与可靠性，为长期操作任务中不确定性对象管理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Robots executing long-horizon manipulation tasks from natural-language instructions must reason about both semantic task structure and geometric feasibility. However, under partial observability, the availability of goal-relevant objects may be uncertain. In such cases, approaches that combine Vision-Language Models (VLMs) with Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) may generate subgoals that rely on the VLM's prior knowledge without observational support, leading to execution failures or unintended outcomes. We propose Evidence Acquisition and Feasibility Gating (EAFG), a framework that acquires visual evidence through VLM-generated exploratory subgoals and TAMP-based execution. EAFG then applies a feasibility gate to decide whether to proceed with task planning, acquire further evidence, or halt. Our experiments show that, in cooking tasks with ambiguous object use, EAFG improves recipe completion by discovering task-relevant objects before planning. For instructions requiring an absent object, EAFG promotes appropriate halt decisions and reduces repeated attempts to manipulate that object.

</details>


### [90] [Towards Professional Tennis Styles for Humanoid Robots with Adaptive Motion Planning and Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20087)
*Tao Huang,Ruofei Liu,Xuchen Tang,Xinyin Zhang,Junli Ren,Huayi Wang,Feiyu Jia,Yukai Qi,Kangning Yin,Weishuai Zeng,Lipeng Chen,Xi Li,Ting Wu,Kailin Li,Ruoli Dai,Jingbo Wang,Lei Han,Jiangmiao Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AdaPT框架，从广播视频学习专业网球发球和底线技术风格，通过自适应运动规划与跟踪的分层设计弥合sim-to-real差距，并在Unitree G1和全尺寸Dobot Atom人形机器人上进行了真实世界验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在真实球类运动上已展现出潜力，但如何在保持强任务性能的同时实现专业级运动风格仍然是一个挑战。此外，仿真中有效的规划跟踪分层设计在真实机器人上存在显著的sim-to-real差距：跟踪性能退化且被自回归规划部分忽视，同时被噪声感知进一步加剧。

Method: 提出AdaPT（自适应运动规划与跟踪框架）：1) 分层设计，规划器生成风格化运动学动作，跟踪器以最小干扰执行；2) 适应机制通过学习跟踪随机化执行速度来提升跟踪鲁棒性；3) 在规划器上引入学习的运动速度适配器以缓解复合误差问题。

Result: 在Unitree G1上进行真实实验证明了适应机制在弥合sim-to-real差距方面的有效性；并将AdaPT策略部署到全尺寸Dobot Atom人形机器人（1.7m）上，实现了无需动作捕捉的野外发球。

Conclusion: 真实世界实验揭示了未来人形球类运动系统的算法与工程见解，验证了所提出的适应性机制在桥接仿真到现实差距方面的有效性。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have recently demonstrated promising capabilities in real-world ball sports. However, achieving professional motion styles while maintaining strong task performance remains challenging. In this work, we propose AdaPT, an Adaptive Motion Planning and Tracking framework that learns professional tennis serving and rally styles directly from broadcast videos. This hierarchical design is motivated by the key insight that the planner generates stylistic kinematic motions, while the tracker executes them with minimal interference with planning. Despite its effectiveness in simulation, a substantial sim-to-real gap emerges: tracking performance inevitably degrades on real robots, and this degradation is partially overlooked by autoregressive planning and further compounded by noisy perception. To address these issues, our adaptation mechanism improves tracking robustness by learning to track randomized execution speeds, while conditioning the planner on a learned motion-speed adapter to mitigate compounding errors. Real-world experiments on the Unitree G1 demonstrate the effectiveness of our adaptation mechanism in bridging the sim-to-real gap. We further deploy AdaPT policies on the full-size Dobot Atom humanoid robot (1.7m) and demonstrate in-the-wild serving without motion capture. Beyond these results, our real-world experiments reveal both algorithmic and engineering insights for future humanoid ball-sports systems. Videos and code are available on our \href{https://humanoidtennis.github.io/AdaPT/}{project website}.

</details>


### [91] [Planning-Oriented End-to-End Autonomous Driving: Architectures, Evaluation, and Emerging Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20111)
*Yanchen Guan,Xingcheng Liu,Bin Rao,Chengyue Wang,Guofa Li,Yunjian Li,Lishengsa Yue,Zhiyong Cui,Chengzhong Xu,Zhenning Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本综述系统梳理了端到端自动驾驶从相机到控制的简单回归范式，向以规划为核心的结构化系统演进的历程，并指出现代端到端驾驶的关键区别不在于是否使用中间表示，而在于这些表示是否被学习、监督和评估以支持安全、可行且符合路线的规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶领域迅速发展，但文献庞杂且评估标准不一致，使得不同方法的进展难以跨基准比较和解释。作者希望通过统一的四轴分析框架（输入表示、规划输出、监督信号、评估协议）来组织文献，澄清这一领域的核心争论。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，沿行为克隆、条件模仿学习、特权蒸馏、BEV与矢量化规划、统一感知-预测-规划架构、世界模型规划器、视觉-语言-动作系统等脉络回顾文献，并沿四个分析轴（输入表示、规划输出、监督信号、评估协议）对现有方法进行综合归纳，同时分析评估基准从开环轨迹匹配到闭环仿真等范式的转变。

Result: 分析表明：仅靠基于位移的开环度量指标无法为安全和人机对齐的驾驶提供充分证据；架构层面的进展在缺乏基准一致的评估时难以被合理解释；评估范式的转变正在推动领域向闭环仿真、非反应性真实日志评估、长尾场景测试和人偏好感知度量演进。

Conclusion: 作者总结了当前面临的开放挑战，包括不确定性感知规划、学习器-专家不匹配、运行时安全保证、语言-动作对齐、世界模型验证以及可复现基准测试等问题，并指出这些方向是未来端到端自动驾驶研究的关键突破口。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving has evolved from camera-to-control regression toward planning-oriented systems that use structured representations, trajectory-level outputs, and increasingly realistic evaluation protocols. This survey reviews this transition across behavior cloning, conditional imitation learning, privileged distillation, BEV and vectorized planning, unified perception-prediction-planning architectures, world-model-based planners, and vision-language-action systems. We argue that the key distinction in modern end-to-end driving is not whether intermediate representations are used, but whether they are learned, supervised, and evaluated to support safe, feasible, and route-compliant planning. To organize the literature, we synthesize existing methods along four axes: input representation, planning output, supervision signal, and evaluation protocol. We further examine the benchmark shift from open-loop trajectory matching to closed-loop simulation, non-reactive real-log evaluation, long-tail testing, and human-preference-aware metrics. Our analysis highlights that architectural progress is difficult to interpret without benchmark-consistent evaluation, and that displacement-based open-loop metrics alone provide limited evidence for safe and human-aligned driving. We conclude with open challenges in uncertainty-aware planning, learner-expert mismatch, runtime safety assurance, language-action grounding, world-model validation, and reproducible benchmarking.

</details>


### [92] [Video2DoorTraversal: Push Door Traversal via Simulated Door Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20251)
*Xincheng Tang,Yiji Chen,Youhan Xie,Wanyu Li,Zhengjie Shu,Lai Jiang,Wenkang Hu,Yitong Li,Jinchuang Zhang,Xibin Song,Ruigang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Video2DoorTraversal，一个基于单视频的实到仿到实框架，用于轮腿式移动操作机器人的开门与穿越任务。它通过DoorTwin重建与真实门实例对齐的关节仿真门，用仿真回路智能体将关节信息转化为参数化技能程序并迭代生成可执行演示，训练ArticuACT双深度策略预测协调的基座、手臂和夹爪命令。系统在五扇真实门上平均成功率96.57%，零样本成功率80.95%，平均约13秒完成整个流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开门并穿越是长时程的移动操作任务，需要精确的把手交互与基座-手臂协调控制，但此前缺乏一个能从单段真实视频出发、端到端完成从感知到策略部署的完整框架。

Method: 提出Video2DoorTraversal框架，包含三个核心组件：1) DoorTwin，从单个RGB视频重建与真实门实例对齐、带关节且可用于仿真的门孪生模型（含真实几何与外观）；2) 仿真回路智能体，将恢复的关节信息转为参数化技能程序，并对失败的滚动进行迭代精化以生成物理可执行的演示；3) ArticuACT双深度策略，使用机器人中心相机条件与交互感知监督，预测协调的基座、手臂和夹爪指令。

Result: 在五扇真实门上平均成功率为96.57%，在结构相似的未见过的门上零样本成功率达80.95%，平均约13秒完成完整的接近、开门和穿越序列。所有感知和策略推断均在机载运行。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了单视频实到仿到实框架在长时程轮腿移动操作定位任务（门开启与穿越）上的有效性，兼具高成功率与零样本泛化能力，且全流程可机载实时运行。

Abstract: Door opening and traversal is a long-horizon loco-manipulation task that requires precise handle interaction and coordinated base-arm control. We present Video2DoorTraversal, a single-video real-to-sim-to-real framework for wheel-legged mobile manipulators. Given one RGB video of a real door, DoorTwin reconstructs an instance-aligned, articulated, and simulation-ready door twin with realistic geometry and appearance. A simulation-in-the-loop agent converts the recovered articulation into a parameterized skill program and iteratively refines failed rollouts to generate physically executable demonstrations. These demonstrations are used to train ArticuACT, a dual-depth policy that predicts coordinated base, arm, and gripper commands using robot-centric camera conditioning and interaction-aware supervision. With all perception and policy inference running onboard, the system achieves a 96.57% average success rate across five real doors and an 80.95% zero-shot success rate on structurally similar unseen doors, while completing the full approach, opening, and traversal sequence in approximately 13s on average. Project Page: https://video2doortraversal.github.io/.

</details>


### [93] [DART-S: Reachability-Audited Active-Suspension Preconditioning for Off-Road Vehicle Jumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20275)
*Yu Hu,Fangzhou Zhao,Liang Chen,Chen Min,Wei Li,Mingyuan Sang,Jiajia Ma,Shican Chen,Di Pang,Baolei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DART-S通过斜坡面预置预压技术，在起飞前改变俯仰角、俯仰速率和车轮自旋状态，从而重新分配扭矩反作用力矩的权限预算。在72个独立BeamNG会话的600次新运行中，所有查询分支均遵循预设路径，在40°/13 m/s边界条件下达到24/24姿态标准成功率（对比DART的0/24），且600次运行中传动系统硬限制超限为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DART（原始方法）仅依赖起飞后的空中扭矩反作用来纠正起飞误差，但其能力受限于轮角动量预算。当起飞初始误差超出该预算时，DART无法成功恢复，因此需要在起飞前对状态进行预置，以扩大或转移剩余权限窗口。

Method: DART-S采用三项关键技术：(1) 斜坡面悬架预压，在起飞前主动改变俯仰角、俯仰速率和轮自旋，从而改变查询状态并调整剩余扭矩权限预算；(2) 局部标定映射，用于预测每次悬架动作如何重塑状态-预算配对；(3) 支持感知选择器，结合预测偏移与局部结果证据以及区间可达性筛选，并通过精确配对审计报告残余权限。

Result: 在72个独立BeamNG会话的600次新运行中，所有正、负、边界查询均遵循预设分支。在40°/13 m/s边界条件下，DART-S实现24/24触地后姿态标准成功（DART仅0/24，会话级Holm调整p=0.0234）；在11.5 m/s下，0.35秒定时动作实现23/24成功（静态预设0/24，p=0.0156）；200 rad/s指令保护使600次运行中传动系统硬限制超限保持为零。

Conclusion: DART-S通过起飞前悬架预置策略，将权限管理从纯空中反应扩展到包含起飞前状态调节，显著提升了跳台起飞误差恢复能力，同时通过指令保护机制确保系统安全性。源代码将在GitHub上公开。

Abstract: Airborne torque reaction cannot recover takeoff errors beyond the wheel angular-momentum budget. DART-S applies ramp-face suspension preconditioning to change pitch, pitch rate, and wheel spin before liftoff, thereby shifting the queried state and altering the remaining authority budget. To predict how each suspension action reshapes this state-budget pair, DART-S employs a local calibration map. A support-aware selector combines the predicted shift with local outcome evidence and an interval-reachability screen; an exact-pair audit reports residual authority. Across 600 new runs in 72 independent BeamNG sessions, every positive, negative, and boundary query follows its prespecified branch. At the confirmed 40°/13 m/s boundary, DART-S attains 24/24 post-touchdown attitude-criterion successes versus 0/24 for DART (session-level Holm-adjusted p=0.0234). At 11.5 m/s, a 0.35 s timing action attains 23/24 versus 0/24 for the static preset (p=0.0156). The 200 rad/s command guard keeps drivetrain hard-limit exceedance at zero across all 600 runs. The source code will be available at https://github.com/MeridianCAS/DART-S

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [Active Inference as Context Acquisition for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19202)
*Sanchayan Dutta,Sai Niranjan Ramachandran,Suvrit Sra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将交互式AI智能体的上下文获取问题形式化为主动推断（active inference）过程，通过内层推断更新潜在任务状态的信念，外层决策在澄清提问、检索、工具调用等上下文中动作和任务动作之间做出权衡，以最小化带成本约束的期望自由能。在确定性设定下，认知项退化为（可标准化为token成本的）期望信息增益。作者实例化为最优提问框架（OQA），使用精确后验和动态规划最优仲裁器，在25至300个候选的多类分类任务上评测前沿语言模型，并研究了生成前澄清和token预算下的自动提示优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式AI智能体需要在获取正确上下文时尽可能高效。当用户省略约束、偏好、文件或任务变量时，智能体面临选择：默认假设或花费token进行澄清、检索、工具调用。现有方法缺乏对这种权衡的统一定量框架，本文旨在弥补这一空白。

Method: 将上下文获取问题形式化为主动推断：内层推断步骤更新对潜在任务状态（latent task state）的信念，外层决策在下一上下文动作、任务动作或停止动作之间选择，以在成本约束下最小化期望自由能。确定性设定下认知项简化为期望信息增益（可用token成本归一化）。实例化为最优提问框架（OQA），采用精确后验和动态规划最优仲裁器。

Result: 在25到300个候选的二元和多类别分类任务上对前沿语言模型进行了基准测试，并研究了生成前澄清与token预算下的自动提示优化。展示了该框架在各类任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该公式是模型无关（model-agnostic）的，将主动推断视为AI智能体上下文获取层的设计原则，为智能体如何在信息获取成本与决策质量之间取得最优平衡提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Interactive AI agents must acquire the right context as efficiently as possible. When a user omits a constraint, preference, file, or task variable, an agent can proceed with a default assumption or spend tokens on a clarifying question, retrieval call, tool call, or prompt trial. We formulate this tradeoff as active inference for context acquisition. An inner inference step updates beliefs over a latent task state, and an outer decision selects the next context action, task action, or stop action to minimize expected free energy under cost. In deterministic settings, the epistemic term reduces to expected information gain, optionally normalized by token cost. We instantiate the framework in Optimal Question Asking (OQA), with exact posteriors and a dynamic programming oracle, and benchmark frontier language models on binary and multiway categorical tasks from 25 to 300 candidates. We also study clarification before generation and automated prompt optimization under token budgets. The formulation is model-agnostic and views active inference as a design principle for the context-acquisition layer of AI agents.

</details>


### [95] [Robust Metaheuristics under Uncertainty for Berth Allocation and Quay Crane Assignment: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19214)
*Yang Li,Peilan Xu,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对不确定性条件下基于种群的鲁棒元启发式算法在泊位分配与岸桥调度问题(BACAP)中的应用进行了系统性综述，从不确定性来源、方法机制归类、基准测试套件三个维度展开，并指出了未来研究挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BACAP是港口运输与货运物流中的代表性调度问题，在实际执行中面临到港偏差、作业时长波动和资源中断等不确定性，使名义最优的调度方案在执行时变得脆弱。尽管基于种群的元启发式算法已被广泛用于BACAP，但现有研究在不确定性表示、鲁棒性准则、搜索机制和实验评估协议等方面零散不统一，缺乏系统性的梳理与总结。

Method: 本文采用机制导向的综述方法：首先总结BACAP中的不确定性来源及其信息表示方式；然后从四个机制维度整理现有方法，包括解表示与解码、鲁棒评估与选择、鲁棒性引导的搜索动态、可行性保持与恢复；随后提出一个面向不确定BACAP的基准测试套件，并通过组合代表性元启发式算法与不同鲁棒性策略报告了基线实验结果。

Result: 据作者所知，这是首篇聚焦于不确定性条件下鲁棒种群元启发式BACAP的专门综述。文章提供了不确定BACAP基准套件及基线实验结果，为受控的实证比较提供了支撑。

Conclusion: 作者指出开放挑战包括：基准扩展、鲁棒性感知的搜索设计、时间自适应鲁棒性以及非平稳不确定性应对等问题，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: The berth allocation and quay crane assignment problem (BACAP) is a representative port-terminal scheduling problem in maritime transportation and freight logistics, where vessel arrivals, berth positions, service durations, and quay?crane availability are tightly coupled. Under uncertainties such as arrival deviations, handling-time fluctuations, and resource disruptions, schedules optimized under nominal assumptions may become fragile during execution, motivating the study of robust metaheuristic optimization for BACAP in port-terminal operations. Although population-based metaheuristics have been widely used for BACAP and related port-scheduling problems, existing studies remain fragmented in their uncertainty repre?sentations, robustness criteria, search mechanisms, and empir?ical evaluation protocols. To the best of our knowledge, this paper provides the first focused review dedicated to robust population-based metaheuristics for BACAP under uncertainty. We first summarize uncertainty sources and information repre?sentations in BACAP, and then organize existing methods from a mechanism-oriented perspective, covering solution representation and decoding, robust evaluation and selection, robustness-guided search dynamics, and feasibility preservation and recovery. We further present a benchmark suite for uncertain BACAP to support controlled empirical comparison and report illustrative baseline results by combining representative metaheuristics with different robustness strategies. Finally, we identify open chal?lenges related to benchmark extension, robustness-aware search design, time-adaptive robustness, and non-stationary uncertainty.

</details>


### [96] [How to Navigate Uncertainty About AI Consciousness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19215)
*Dr Tom McClelland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 面对人工智能意识问题的深层不确定性，本文提出将研究焦点从难以解决的AI意识问题转向可解决的AI效价（valence）问题，通过评估AI是否具有在意识存在时构成效价体验的状态，为潜在的感知AI开发建立负责任的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI是否具有意识的深层不确定性下，存在两难困境：假定无感知可能伤害有道德地位的实体，假定有感知又可能浪费资源于无感知机器。由于AI意识问题本身难以解决，需要寻找摆脱这一困境的路径。

Method: 提出将问题的焦点从不可解的"AI意识"问题转向可解的"AI效价"问题，即评估AI是否具有在意识存在时构成效价体验（如痛苦、愉悦）的状态，以此作为负责任AI开发的道德基础。

Result: 论证了评估AI效价状态足以支撑一种负责任的方法，用于应对潜在有感知AI的开发与对待问题。

Conclusion: 通过将关注的焦点从AI意识转向AI效价，可以绕开意识问题的不可解性，为潜在的感知AI提供可操作的伦理决策框架。

Abstract: Given deep uncertainty about the possibility of artificial consciousness, it is unclear how we should treat potentially sentient AI. On the one hand, we could assume insentience but risk doing terrible harms to entities that deserve moral standing. On the other hand, we could assume sentience and instead risk wasting resources on insentient machines. The intractability of questions around AI consciousness mean that this dilemma is hard to escape. I suggest a way out of that shifts from intractable questions of AI consciousness to tractable questions of AI valence. Specifically, we can assess whether an AI has states that would constitute valenced experiences if it were conscious. I show how this is sufficient to ground a responsible approach to the development of potentially conscious AI.

</details>


### [97] [Bounded Sovereignty and the Control Tax: Pricing AI Oversight When the Deployer Does Not Own the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19216)
*Zhen Wen Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"有界主权"（bounded sovereignty）概念，用于刻画受监管组织通过API或托管端点使用前沿模型时对AI栈各层实际拥有的技术性和契约性访问权限。该访问条件决定了哪些AI控制协议能在实践中真正执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI控制研究假设部署者能够完全掌控模型及其周边管线（权重、基础设施、日志等），但受监管组织通过API使用前沿模型时通常不具备这些访问权限，导致大量控制协议在实践上不可执行。

Method: 提出四层访问类型学（数据、模型、基础设施、交互层），构建协议-分层需求矩阵，定义主权折扣成本（sovereignty discount cost）概念，并通过135万条合成案例模拟进行访问消融实验，配合匿名的国家支付基础设施场景进行解释性分析。

Result: 实验表明：完整日志改善诊断能力；执行前网关支持干预；轨迹访问与模型版本控制增强事后解释；范围限制可在改善安全性的同时降低有用性。结果验证了访问条件对控制协议执行的决定性影响。

Conclusion: 控制协议作为通用安全方案时应明确陈述其访问假设。该研究是构念效度（construct-validity）练习而非真实支付系统证据。

Abstract: AI control research asks how to deploy models safely even when they may be misaligned, but many control protocols assume that the deployer can instrument the model and its surrounding pipeline. That assumption often fails for regulated organisations using frontier models through APIs or managed endpoints, where the deployer may control the business process but not the model weights, serving infrastructure, internal traces, update process, or full interaction logs. This paper introduces bounded sovereignty: partial technical and contractual access across the data, model, infrastructure, and interaction layers of the AI stack. It argues that these access conditions determine which control protocols can be executed in practice. The paper contributes a four-layer access typology, a protocol-by-layer requirements matrix, and the concept of sovereignty discount cost: the part of the control tax spent substituting for missing access through contracts, architecture, audit, vendor assurance, residual risk, or reduced system scope. It also reports a synthetic access-ablation experiment over 1.35 million synthetic case simulations and interprets the findings through an anonymised national-payments-infrastructure scenario. The experiment is not real-world payment-system evidence; it is a construct-validity exercise. The results show that complete logs improve diagnosis, a pre-execution gateway enables intervention, trace access and model-version control strengthen post-incident explanation, and scope restriction can improve safety while reducing usefulness. Control protocols proposed as general safety solutions should therefore state their access assumptions explicitly.

</details>


### [98] [Interaction valence reveals contrasting social networks in dairy cattle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19222)
*Sibi Parivendan,Suresh Raja Neethirajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种情感感知（valence-aware）的社会网络分析框架，将基于姿态的计算机视觉管道从商业奶牛场挤奶前区域连续视频中提取的交互事件，转化为群体层面上的亲和性与对抗性组织表征。质检后保留36头牛、177个二元对的1,183个交互；审计样本中自动与人工标签一致率为82.8%，宏观F1为0.872。聚合网络连通且预测的亲和事件形成五个算法社群（模块度Q=0.429）。按预测情感分离事件后，亲和与对抗层在边集、社群划分和个体位置上呈现描述性差异，表明汇总的交互计数可能掩盖观测网络的行为组成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会关系影响资源获取、冲突暴露和群体稳定性，但现有自动化牲畜监测通常将行为视为孤立事件，缺乏将交互转化为群体层面社会网络结构的框架，因此需要一种能够区分亲和与对抗价态的网络分析方法。

Method: 采用姿态基计算机视觉管道分析7小时39分钟来自商业奶牛场挤奶前区域的连续视频；经过质量控制在1,414个候选交互中保留1,183个，涉及36头牛和177个二元对；对198个管道检测片段进行预测类别平衡审计，比较自动与人工标签；构建聚合网络并计算密度、传递性、平均路径长度、社群模块度及个体中心性；按预测情感分为亲和与对抗两个网络层进行对比分析。

Result: 审计样本中自动与人工标签一致率为82.8%，未加权宏观F1为0.872；聚合网络连通（密度0.281，传递性0.513，平均路径长度1.88）；预测亲和事件形成五个算法社群（Q=0.429）；预测对抗交互占保留事件的72.4%和交互时长的76.0%；伙伴最多的牛并非介数中心性最高者；按价态分离后亲和与对抗层面在边集、社群和个体位置上差异显著。

Conclusion: 情感感知的网络分析能够揭示汇总交互计数所掩盖的行为组成差异，为检验竞争、亲和及福利相关变化的假设提供框架；但在作为福利或健康指标使用前需进行纵向验证。作者同时强调审计结果描述的是审计样本本身，而非患病率加权或端到端的部署性能。

Abstract: Social relationships shape access to resources, exposure to conflict and group stability, yet automated livestock monitoring typically treats behaviour as isolated events. Here, we present a valence-aware social-network framework that transforms video-derived interactions into herd-level representations of affiliative and agonistic organization. A pose-based computer-vision pipeline analysed 7 h 39 min of continuous video from the pre-milking area of one commercial dairy farm. After quality control, 1,183 of 1,414 candidate interactions remained, involving 36 cows and 177 dyads. In a predicted-class-balanced audit of 198 pipeline-detected clips, automated and manual labels agreed in 82.8% of cases, with an unweighted audit-sample macro-F1 of 0.872. These values describe the audited sample rather than prevalence-weighted or end-to-end deployment performance. The aggregated network was connected (density = 0.281; transitivity = 0.513; mean path length = 1.88), and predicted affiliative events formed five algorithmic communities (modularity Q = 0.429). Within the observed zone, predicted agonistic interactions comprised 72.4% of retained events and 76.0% of interaction duration. The cow with the most partners did not have the highest betweenness centrality. Separating events by predicted valence produced descriptively different affiliative and agonistic layers, with contrasting edge sets, community partitions and individual positions. Thus, pooled interaction counts can obscure the behavioural composition of an observed network. Valence-aware analysis provides a framework for testing hypotheses about competition, affiliation and welfare-relevant change, while requiring longitudinal validation before use as a welfare or health indicator.

</details>


### [99] [Air Traffic Control Using Large Language Models: Prompt Engineering, Architecture, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19299)
*Mahyar Ghazanfari,Matthias Casanova,Jordan Kam,Alex Zongo,Peng Wei,Torsten Darrell,Alexandre Bayen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实验评估了大语言模型能否生成具有操作真实性的空中交通管制（ATC）通话。以旧金山"Bay Tour"航线的通用航空飞行转录为基准，设计了五种约束递增的提示结构，并在九种开源和闭源LLM上进行多轮对话评估。结果表明提供工作示例能提升相似度，但收紧提示反而效果下降，最严格的脚本化提示因自身错误累积而崩溃，注入正确历史可以修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中交通管制通信是安全至上的对话任务，在空域管理其他环节已半自动化的情况下，ATC对话仍基本依赖人工。作者希望探索LLM在该高安全性场景下生成符合操作现实的ATC指令的能力，为未来LLM辅助ATC系统提供实验依据与路径参考。

Method: 以一次手动转录的通用航空"Bay Tour"航班通话为基准（P0），通过飞行员参与过程设计五种约束程度递增的提示结构（P1-P5），并嵌入有状态的多轮对话管线中，让模型扮演管制员角色。在九种开源和闭源LLM上，系统性地变化提示结构、是否提供另一实验航班的工作转录作为上下文示例、以及模型是基于自身先前回复还是注入的真实历史进行条件生成。采用词汇、结构和语义相似度指标以及经过人类专家标注验证的LLM-as-judge（GPT-5.5）进行评分。

Result: 提供工作示例能提升生成文本的相似度，但收紧提示约束并不能带来改进：最轻量的提示表现最佳，而最重脚本化的提示因自身错误在对话中累积而性能崩溃，这一崩溃可通过注入正确历史得到修复。

Conclusion: 这些结果勾勒出LLM辅助ATC的一条具体可行路径，同时揭示了当前技术在此安全关键场景下的局限性。

Abstract: Air traffic control (ATC) communication is a safety-critical dialogue that remains largely human-driven even as other parts of air traffic management have been semi-automated. In this article, we experimentally evaluate whether large language models (LLMs) can generate operationally realistic ATC transmissions. An experimental general-aviation flight flying over the San Francisco "Bay Tour" route is hand-transcribed and used as ground truth (P0). Through a pilot-in-the-loop process we design five prompt structures (P1-P5) of increasing constraint and embed them in a stateful multi-turn pipeline, where the model plays ATC to a fixed pilot transcript while conditioning on the accumulating dialogue history. Across nine open- and closed-source LLMs we vary the prompt, the presence of a worked transcript from a different experimental flight as an in-context example, and whether the model conditions on its own prior replies or on injected ground-truth history. Turns are scored with lexical, structural, and semantic similarity metrics and by an LLM-as-judge (GPT-5.5) validated against human expert annotation. Supplying a worked example improves similarity, but tightening the prompt does not: the lightest prompts perform best and the most heavily scripted one collapses as its own errors accumulate through the dialogue, which injecting correct history repairs. These results outline a concrete path and its current limits toward LLM-assisted ATC.

</details>


### [100] [Outcome Monitors: Recovery Affordances for Silent Tool Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19303)
*Sugam Panthi,Rabab Abdelfattah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Outcome Monitors机制，用于检测工具调用结果（tool call outcomes）违反"结果契约"（outcome contracts）的情况——即响应格式正确但内容错误（如缓存错误页或负价格）。在违反时，监控器保留结果并发布非约束性"回执"（receipt），指明违反的属性及公开恢复工具。实验表明，在ToolMaze（完成率从10.9%提升至28.1%）和tau-bench零售（提升14.0与12.0个百分点）中显著改善；消融实验证明恢复工具列表是效果的关键来源，而诊断细节与时间无影响；结果违反可被检测的范围受限于挖掘的契约词汇表之外时，检测率降至46%，但交付仍继续、完成率不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究中，agent对工具调用超时等显式失败能感知并绕行，但当缓存错误页或负价格等"格式正确但内容错误"的结果出现时，agent会将其当作事实消费，导致任务失败。这类隐式失败缺乏检测机制，需要一种能识别结果契约违规的手段。

Method: 1) 提出Outcome Monitors：从任务不相交的轨迹（task-disjoint traces）中挖掘或从公开schema推导"结果契约"（outcome contracts），用以检测结果是否违反契约；2) 检测到违规后，保留原始结果，并发布非约束性回执，回执中指明被违反的属性及公开恢复工具（recovery tools）；3) 在冻结的预置评估中注入故障，进行多模型、多提供商家族的实验及系列消融控制实验（移除恢复工具列表、改变诊断细节与时机等）。

Result: 1) ToolMaze上，四个模型、两个提供商家族中完成率从10.9%提升至28.1%，并在第三个提供商家族中复现；2) tau-bench零售中，两个层级分别提升14.0和12.0个百分点；3) 消融实验显示：移除恢复工具列表使增益消失，恢复后效果复原，而诊断细节和时机无显著差异；4) 收益集中在故障阻断完成的地方；5) 在转录自公开事故分类法的测试套件上，检测落在已挖掘词汇表之外时的检测率降至46%，但交付持续、完成率不变，表明检测范围扩展仍是开放问题。

Conclusion: Outcome Monitors能够有效检测"格式正确但语义错误"的工具结果，并通过回执中的恢复工具引导agent绕行，显著提升多基准上的任务完成率。效果主要来自恢复工具列表这一回执内容，而非诊断细节或时机。当前局限在于检测依赖挖掘的契约词汇表，词汇表之外的违规检测率低，扩展检测概念（beyond the contract vocabulary）是未来方向。

Abstract: When a tool call times out, the agent sees the failure and can route around it. A cached error page or negative price can instead arrive in the expected format and be consumed as fact. We introduce Outcome Monitors, which detect violations of outcome contracts mined from task-disjoint traces or derived from public schemas. On a violation, the monitor preserves the result and issues a nonbinding receipt naming the violated property and public recovery tools. In frozen, prespecified evaluations with injected failures, Outcome Monitors raise ToolMaze completion from 10.9% to 28.1% across four models in two provider families and replicate in a third. In tau-bench retail, completion improves by 14.0 and 12.0 points on two tiers. In separate ToolMaze controls, removing the recovery-tool list eliminates the measured gain and restoring it recovers the effect; diagnostic detail and timing produce no detectable differences. Gains concentrate where the fault blocks completion. On a suite transcribed from a published incident taxonomy, detection outside the mined vocabulary falls to 46%, though delivery continues and completion is unchanged. Recovery tools are the active receipt content in these controls; extending detection beyond the contract vocabulary remains open.

</details>


### [101] [Beyond Imitation: Filtering On-Policy Distillation by Reasoning Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19408)
*Chen Yang,Haiyuan Wan,Rengrong Xiong,Yize Chen,Danny H. K. Tsang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出R2-OPD方法，针对On-policy蒸馏中教师奖励与真实推理进展不一致的问题，通过构建轨迹内推理跨度排名对比，在排名不一致时抑制蒸馏奖励，从而减少有冲突的监督信号，提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy蒸馏（OPD）假设教师奖励能恰当反映推理进展，但在实践中这一假设不成立：推理步骤即使具有清晰的推理推进，也可能因偏离教师输出而获得较低的蒸馏奖励，导致教师奖励与真实推理进展相冲突。

Method: 提出R2-OPD（Reasoning-Progress-Aware Reward Filtering for On-Policy Distillation）：构建两个轨迹内推理跨度排名——一个基于教师奖励，另一个基于独立估计的进展奖励；当两个排名不一致时，选择性抑制蒸馏奖励，从而在减少与推理进展冲突的监督的同时保留有效的教师引导。

Result: R2-OPD在推理性能上相较于标准OPD方法展现出持续一致的改进，尤其在推理相关任务的评分上表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过感知推理进展的奖励过滤机制，可以有效缓解教师奖励与推理进展之间的不匹配问题，在不丢失有效教师引导的前提下提升学生模型的推理能力。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as an effective framework for post-training language models by pairing student-generated trajectories with dense token-level supervision from a teacher. However, OPD implicitly assumes that teacher-derived rewards are an appropriate proxy for reasoning progress, and therefore treats all teacher feedback equally during policy optimization. While in practice, this assumption does not always hold. We observe that teacher-derived rewards often conflict with genuine reasoning progress, as reasoning steps with clear reasoning advancement may still receive lower distillation rewards, simply due to deviation from teacher's outputs. To address this mismatch, we propose Reasoning-Progress-Aware Reward Filtering for On-Policy Distillation (R2-OPD), which constructs two within-trajectory rankings of reasoning spans, one from teacher-derived rewards and the other from independently estimated progress reward. Distillation rewards are selectively suppressed whenever the two rankings disagree, reducing supervision that conflicts with reasoning progress while preserving effective teacher guidance. Our approach shows consistent improvement over standard OPD especially regarding reasoning performances.

</details>


### [102] [Symposium: Trust via Auditable Records for Communities of AI Scientist Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19511)
*Dexter Pratt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Symposium是一个形式化框架及其实践实现，旨在为小型科学研究社区中部署的AI智能体提供长期、不可篡改的操作记录，形成可审计的科研活动历史轨迹，包括分析、假设、数据和科学讨论，从而支持智能体基于先前工作进行构建，并保留科研人员和智能体进行信任评估所需的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体在科研社区中的广泛应用，需要一个将科学社区的持久历史与运行于其上的智能体分离的框架，以便在快速演变的多AI系统环境中保留可审计的科研记录，并使信任评估成为可能。

Method: Symposium设计为一个独立于AI智能体或集成研究环境的框架，提供发布基础设施、智能体提示组件和文档，用于记录结构化科学论证，包括主张、细粒度证据引用、假设以及关于哪些材料可作为证据的明确声明。

Result: 提供了可工作的实现，包括发布基础设施、智能体提示组件和文档，使用户能够快速建立并运行自己的Symposium社区。

Conclusion: Symposium通过将社区的持久历史与操作该历史的AI系统分离，为小型科研社区提供了长期、不可篡改的AI智能体操作记录，从而支持可审计的科研活动和目的依赖的信任评估。

Abstract: Symposium is a formal framework and practical implementation to record the operation of AI agents deployed by small scientific research communities. Symposium provides long-term, immutable histories of agent-driven research activity, leaving auditable trails of analyses, hypotheses, data, and scientific discourse. This shared record of published artifacts enables agents to build on prior work and preserves the evidence researchers and agents need to make purpose-dependent trust assessments. Symposium captures scientific argument, including structured claims, fine-grained evidence citations, assumptions, and explicit declarations of what material may and may not be used as evidence. Symposium differs from AI co-scientist agents or integrated AI research environments; it is a framework that separates a scientific community's durable history from the agents and other systems that operate on that history. It assumes that a community will use diverse AI systems in a rapidly evolving environment. A working implementation of the publication infrastructure, agent prompt components, and documentation are provided to enable users to rapidly set up and run their own Symposium community.

</details>


### [103] [From Retrieved Context to Runtime Control: Adaptive Compression for Edge-based RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19535)
*Zlatan Feric,Amir Taherin,Yanzhi Wang,David Kaeli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向边缘RAG的遥测感知自适应上下文压缩方案。通过在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor上使用Llama/Qwen生成器、Natural Questions/HotpotQA数据集和LLMLingua-2压缩器进行实验表征，发现生成过程占据RAG预算约90%的延迟和91%的GPU能耗。中间压缩率可在质量损失可忽略的前提下减少高达53.2%的GPU能耗和48.2%的SoC能耗，但温和压缩会错失节能机会，激进压缩会损害推理质量。作者主张采用基于工作负载特征和边缘遥测驱动的运行时动态压缩策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG的上下文压缩方法通常使用固定压缩预算或离线选择压缩率后推理时静态应用，忽视了工作负载变化和边缘设备的实时状态。在边缘SoC上压缩本身并非免费，压缩器与生成器共享同一块SoC资源，其自身消耗的延迟和能耗可能抵消生成阶段节省的开销。因此需要提出一种能够动态适应工作负载和设备状态的压缩策略。

Method: 本文首先在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor边缘SoC上，利用Llama和Qwen生成器、Natural Questions和HotpotQA数据集以及LLMLingua-2压缩器，对RAG推理流程中压缩与生成之间的延迟和能耗权衡进行了系统性表征实验。基于测量结果，作者分析了不同压缩率对延迟、GPU能耗、SoC能耗和生成质量的影响，识别出存在一个自适应运行的中间压缩率区间，并据此论证了结合遥测信息和工作负载特征进行运行时压缩率动态调整的必要性。

Result: 实验结果表明：较大规模模型（7B-8B）的生成阶段占据了单次查询约90%的延迟和91%的GPU能耗。压缩率的影响呈现非单调特性——温和压缩可能无法充分挖掘节能潜力，而过度激进压缩会恶化推理质量。选择中间压缩率可在质量损失可忽略的前提下，将GPU能耗降低最多53.2%，SoC总能耗降低最多48.2%。

Conclusion: 压缩率的选择应当是一个动态决策而非静态预设。作者认为需要设计基于工作负载特征（如查询类型、上下文长度）和边缘设备实时遥测（如SoC功耗、资源利用率等）的运行时策略来动态管理压缩率，以实现RAG在边缘场景下的能效和质量的最佳权衡。本文属于愿景/立场类论文，以实验证据为基础提出未来研究方向。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves language-model responses by grounding generation in external passages, which comes with overhead: retrieved context lengthens the prompt, increasing prefill work, KV-cache footprint, memory traffic, latency, and energy. Context compression offers a natural remedy by pruning retrieved text before generation. However, state-of-the-art context-compression methods are typically used with a fixed compression budget, or with the rate selected offline and then applied at inference time. This static view ignores both workload variation and the live state of the edge device. On an edge SoC, compression is not free: the compressor itself runs on the same SoC and consumes latency and energy that can offset any generation savings.
  This paper proposes a vision for telemetry-informed adaptive compression in edge RAG, grounded in experimental evidence. We characterize the compression tradeoff on the NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor using Llama and Qwen generators, Natural Questions and HotpotQA datasets, and LLMLingua-2 compression. Our measurements show that generation dominates the RAG budget for larger models, reaching roughly 90% of per-query latency and 91% of GPU energy for 7B-8B generators. Exploring the impact of the compression rate reveals an adaptive operating region: mild compression can miss energy opportunities, and overly aggressive compression can hurt inference quality. Intermediate compression can reduce GPU energy by up to 53.2%, and SoC energy by up to 48.2%, with negligible quality loss. We argue for runtime policies that dynamically manage compression, guided by workload features and edge telemetry.

</details>


### [104] [Enforcing LLM Safety through DMD-based Classification of Prompt-Response Embedding Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19579)
*Mohamed Akrout,Olivera Kotevska,Dan Wilson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Koopman动力系统的黑盒LLM安全分类方法，通过分别对安全与不安全输入输出对拟合Koopman预测模型，并利用差分残差分数进行分类。实验表明在三个安全基准上，融合提示词与响应的嵌入动力学能提升效果，尤其在交互依赖型违规场景下表现显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在高风险场景中部署日益广泛，但其可能生成有毒、有害或违反政策的内容。在黑盒条件下高效检测这些不安全输出仍是未解决的挑战，现有方法缺乏对提示词与响应之间交互动态的建模。

Method: 将针对幻觉检测提出的动力系统框架扩展到LLM安全分类。将提示词和响应投影到高维嵌入空间，为安全与非安全两种状态分别拟合基于Koopman算子的预测模型，通过比较两种状态预测误差的差分残差分数对新输出进行分类。关键创新是同时纳入提示词与响应的嵌入动力学，使Koopman算子捕获二者的交互模式。

Result: 在三个安全基准上使用三种嵌入模型进行评估。结果表明：纳入提示词嵌入一致地提升分类性能；对于依赖交互的违规，配合因果解码器（如Llama-3）效果最佳；而对于仅依赖响应的违规，密集语义嵌入表示更有效。

Conclusion: 本文展示了利用动力系统框架分析AI系统的可行性，开创了从"用AI建模动力系统"到"用动力系统分析AI系统"的新范式，为黑盒LLM安全检测提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes applications, yet their tendency to generate toxic, harmful, or policy-violating content poses significant risks. Detecting these unsafe outputs efficiently in a black-box manner remains an open challenge. In this paper, we extend a recently proposed dynamical systems framework designed for hallucination detection to LLM safety classification. By projecting both prompts and responses into high-dimensional embedding spaces and fitting separate Koopman-based predictive models for safe and unsafe regimes, we classify new outputs using a new differential residual score that compares prediction errors of the safe and unsafe regimes. A key contribution is the incorporation of the prompt and response embedding dynamics, yielding fitted Koopman operators that capture crucial interaction patterns. We evaluate our black-box method across three safety benchmarks using three embedding models. Our results show that incorporating prompt embeddings yields consistent improvements, particularly for interaction-dependent violations when paired with causal decoders (e.g., in Llama-3), while response-only violations benefit more from dense semantic embedding representations. These findings opens the door for using dynamical systems to analyze AI systems rather than the dominant paradigm of using AI to model dynamical systems.

</details>


### [105] [Scientific Data Skills: Enabling Agent-Ready Scientific Data Services at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19625)
*Xiaohan Huang,Qingqing Long,Xiaolei Du,Siyu Pu,Jiawen Xu,Haotian Chen,Chenyang Zhao,Jinbiao Liu,Xuezhi Wang,Hao Wang,Hengshu Zhu,Yuanchun Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Scientific Data Skill (SciDSK)，一种面向AI代理的可复用数据集表示方法，将数据集特定知识与操作指南打包为代理技能，并通过Scientific Data Skill Bank平台发布，实验证明其能显著提升代理驱动的数据集发现与解释能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集表示对AI代理的自主发现、解释和调用支持有限，原因在于科学数据分散在异构存储库中，且数据集表示主要为人类使用而设计，难以满足代理自动化的需求。

Method: 定义SciDSK结构规范，包含数据集描述、科学上下文、文件组织、使用流程、质量检查和溯源信息，数据本身保留在原始存储库；构建系统化管护流程，基于权威数据集记录和辅助材料生成SciDSK；建立Scientific Data Skill Bank平台，覆盖六个科学学科，支持包访问、持久标识和源数据集追踪。

Result: 通过数据集发现的检索基准和数据集解释的控制实验评估，结果显示SciDSK改善了代理驱动的数据集发现，并为数据集解释提供了更精确、可操作的支持。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，以代理就绪的表示形式组织数据集特定知识是有价值的，为AI代理与科学数据的高效交互提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Scientific data are increasingly used by AI agents, yet existing dataset representations provide limited support for autonomous discovery, interpretation, and invocation. This limitation stems from the fragmentation of scientific data across heterogeneous repositories and from dataset representations designed primarily for human use. To address this limitation, we introduce the Scientific Data Skill (SciDSK), an agent-ready representation that packages dataset-specific knowledge and operational guidance as a reusable agent skill. A SciDSK integrates dataset descriptions, scientific context, file organization, usage procedures, quality checks, and provenance information while retaining the underlying data in its original repository. We define a structured SciDSK specification and develop a systematic construction pipeline that grounds each SciDSK in authoritative dataset records and associated supporting materials. We further establish the Scientific Data Skill Bank, a unified platform that publishes SciDSK resources across six scientific disciplines and supports package access, persistent identification, and traceability to source datasets. We evaluate SciDSK through a retrieval benchmark for dataset discovery and controlled cases for dataset interpretation. The results show that SciDSK improves agent-driven dataset discovery and provides more precise and actionable support for dataset interpretation. These findings support the value of organizing dataset-specific knowledge in an agent-ready representation.

</details>


### [106] [Can Agent Memory Systems Track Evolving State?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19652)
*Xinyi Fan,Miri Liu,Ruozhen Yang,Siru Ouyang,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出状态跟踪（state tracking）作为LLM智能体记忆系统的新能力基准，发布StateMemBench基准（234个多会话场景），并提出了StateMem状态优先的记忆方法，在准确率上相比基线提升1.8倍，并可作为轻量级单次调用包装器提升现有记忆系统32-67个百分点的当前状态准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准主要聚焦于"回忆"（recall）型任务，但实际中长期和高风险任务中，记忆系统必须跟踪不断演化的世界状态——事实、约束和决策在长交互中被修订时，答案必须反映当前状态而非过期状态。现有研究忽视了这一关键能力缺口。

Method: 1) 定义状态跟踪（state tracking）能力并构建StateMemBench基准，包含234个多会话场景、两种对话长度区间，采用闭池（closed-pool）评分区分当前状态、过时状态和错误；2) 提出StateMem状态优先记忆方法，显式跟踪超驰（supersession）关系和关系依赖；3) 将状态方法作为轻量级单次调用包装器应用于现有记忆系统。

Result: 该任务对现有记忆系统、检索增强基线和长上下文基线均具有挑战性。StateMem在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上将当前状态准确率提升1.8倍（0.205→0.363），在Qwen-3.5-9B上相比最强记忆系统提升1.6倍（0.149→0.233），同时与长上下文基线保持竞争力。作为轻量级包装器时，在六个记忆与检索后端上将当前状态准确率提升32至67个百分点，其中15至32个点归因于状态结构而非增加的上下文。

Conclusion: 状态跟踪是记忆系统的重要但被忽视的能力维度。状态优先的记忆结构（显式跟踪超驰和依赖关系）能显著提升当前状态准确率，并且该思路可以模块化地推广到现有记忆系统作为轻量级增强，其效果主要来自状态结构本身而非额外上下文。

Abstract: As LLM-based agents are deployed for longer and higher-stakes tasks, their memory systems continue to have crucial gaps. While existing memory benchmarks focus largely on recall-shaped tasks, we argue an effective memory system must track the evolving state of the world; as facts, constraints, and decisions are revised over a long interaction, answers must reflect the current state and not a superseded one. We define this capability as state tracking and instantiate it in StateMemBench, a benchmark of 234 multi-session scenarios spanning two conversation-length regimes. Its closed-pool grading scores whether an answer reflects the current state, the superseded state, or fails otherwise, separating state-tracking failures from other errors by construction. Our analysis shows that this task is challenging for existing memory systems, retrieval-augmented baselines, and long-context baselines. We then present StateMem, a state-first memory method that explicitly tracks supersession and relational dependencies, and show it improves current-state accuracy over the strongest same-backbone baseline by 1.8x (0.205 -> 0.363) on DeepSeek-V4-Flash and over the strongest memory system by 1.6x (0.149 -> 0.233) on Qwen-3.5-9B, while remaining competitive with the long-context baselines. Finally, we show the same state approach can be applied as a lightweight single-call wrapper over existing memory systems, lifting current-state accuracy by +32 to +67 points on StateMemBench across six memory and retrieval backends. A length- and cost-matched control attributes +15 to +32 of those points to state structure rather than added context.

</details>


### [107] [Frequency-Aware Continual Learning for Smart Contract Vulnerability Detection with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19680)
*Tenghui Huang,Jiawen Kang,Dongning Liu,Changyan Yi,Chengjun Cai,Anjia Yang,Li Li,Dong In Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个三阶段框架解决LLM在智能合约漏洞检测中的三个级联挑战：频率感知低秩适配（FA-LoRA）实现参数高效适应、遗忘感知回放（FAR）缓解灾难性遗忘、锚保护渐进合并（APPM）将多适配器合并为单一模型。在DIVE数据集上验证，APPM达到0.8085的Micro-F1，接近独立任务上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约漏洞检测中，新的漏洞类别不断出现，全量重训练代价过高；同时持续学习新任务会导致灾难性遗忘，且推理时任务身份未知，多个适配器无法直接使用。这三个挑战因果链式关联，需要统一框架解决。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(1) FA-LoRA在傅里叶域进行参数高效适配，用逐频率重要性门控，仅需0.4%可训练参数；(2) FAR利用频率门控结合损失动态估算逐样本遗忘风险，优先回放脆弱知识；(3) APPM利用FAR训练产生的非对称泛化识别最强泛化适配器作为锚，通过锚保护的加权合并和频域门控竞争将全部分配器合并为单一模型。

Result: FA-LoRA仅需0.4%可训练参数且超越标准LoRA和QLoRA；FAR在顺序任务上达到0.8022平均Micro-F1；APPM达到0.8085 Micro-F1，距独立任务上界仅差2.7%，合并耗时156ms且无额外运行时内存开销。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了智能合约漏洞检测中参数高效适应、灾难性遗忘和适配器合并三个级联挑战，为不断演化的区块链生态提供了完整的持续学习解决方案。

Abstract: Smart contract vulnerability detection with Large Language Models (LLMs) faces three causally linked challenges. First, new vulnerability categories demand parameter-efficient adaptation, since full retraining is prohibitive for sequentially arriving tasks. Second, training per-task adapters on a shared backbone causes catastrophic forgetting of previously learned vulnerabilities. Third, the resulting multiplicity of adapters must be consolidated into a single model, since task identity is unknown at inference time. Each challenge arises directly from the solution to its predecessor, making an integrated framework essential. We propose a three-stage pipeline in which each stage addresses one challenge and feeds into the next. The adaptation stage uses Frequency-Aware Low-Rank Adaptation (FA-LoRA), which performs adaptation in the Fourier domain with per-frequency importance gates, requiring only 0.4% trainable parameters while outperforming standard LoRA and QLoRA. The continual learning stage applies Forget-Aware Replay (FAR), which uses these frequency gates to estimate per-sample forgetting risk via loss dynamics and prioritizes vulnerable knowledge for rehearsal, achieving an average Micro-F1 of 0.8022 across sequential tasks. The deployment stage employs Anchor-Protected Progressive Merging (APPM), which exploits the asymmetric generalization produced by FAR training to identify the strongest-generalizing adapter as an anchor and consolidates all adapters into a single model via anchor-protected weighted merging with frequency-domain gate competition. APPM achieves a Micro-F1 of 0.8085, within 2.7% of the independent per-task upper bound, at a merge cost of 156 ms and no additional runtime memory. Experiments on DIVE confirm the framework effectively addresses all three challenges for evolving blockchain ecosystems.

</details>


### [108] [Learning Hierarchical Skill Policies with Offline Quality-Diversity Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19684)
*Tanachai Anakewat,Takayuki Osa,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QDOS是一个统一的离线到在线学习框架，通过优势加权质量多样性预训练目标从离线数据集中提取多样且高价值的技能，并结合双重数据集复用策略，在稀疏奖励的结构化操控和非结构化运动任务中显著超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两阶段离线技能学习方法（如轨迹VAE）的低层策略质量高度依赖数据集质量，当数据集质量不佳时，提取的技能质量也会下降，限制了后续高层策略的任务求解能力。

Method: 提出优势加权质量多样性（Advantage-Weighted Quality-Diversity）预训练目标，用每个轨迹片段的估计优势值对技能提取和多样性目标进行加权；同时引入双重数据集复用策略，将离线数据既用于技能预训练，又通过伪标签填充在线回放缓冲区。

Result: 在结构化操控任务和非结构化运动任务上的实验表明，QDOS显著优于强基线方法，能够加速探索并提高稀疏奖励场景下的最终回报。

Conclusion: 通过优势加权的质量多样性学习与双重数据集复用策略，QDOS提供了鲁棒且任务相关的技能表征，有效缓解了低质量离线数据对技能提取的负面影响，实现了更高效的离线到在线强化学习。

Abstract: Recent studies investigate how to leverage pre-collected datasets to improve the policy performance and sample efficiency of RL. One promising approach to achieve this goal is to employ a two-stage strategy: In the first stage, diverse skills are extracted as a low-level policy from a given dataset, and a high-level policy is trained to solve a specific task in the second stage. Typically, extraction of the low-level policy is performed based on unsupervised learning such as trajectory VAE. However, a limitation of this approach is that the quality of the low-level policy highly depends on the quality of the dataset. To address this issue, we introduce QDOS (Quality-Diversity Offline Skill learning), a unified pipeline for robust offline-to-online learning. Our approach incorporates an Advantage-Weighted Quality-Diversity pretraining objective, which weights the skill extraction and diversity objectives by the estimated advantage of each trajectory segment. This approach allows the model to extract diverse and high-value skills. By providing robust and task-relevant skill representations, QDOS significantly improves the quality of the embedded skill space used by the low-level policy. We further integrate this with a dual dataset reuse strategy, where offline data is used both for skill pretraining and for populating the online replay buffer via pseudo-labeling. Experiments demonstrate that QDOS significantly outperforms strong baselines in structured manipulation tasks and unstructured locomotion tasks, confirming its ability to accelerate exploration and improve final returns in challenging sparse-reward domains.

</details>


### [109] [Rethinking the Evaluation and Optimization of LLM-Based Social Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19689)
*Pei Wang,Xu Chen,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出以人类行为的主观性为核心，指出基于准确率的评估与硬标签训练在LLM社会仿真中的缺陷，引入主观性系数（subjectivity coefficient）刻画任务主观程度，并提出SALT方法（基于主观性自适应的软标签训练），同时构建了包含19,300个上下文、193名标注者、100个主观问题的SUBJSIM基准，在单观测训练、全分布评估的现实设定下验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM社会仿真是传统调查和行为实验的有力补充，但当前评估与训练方式存在根本缺陷：传统实践用准确率衡量（模型是否选出人类观察到的单一响应）并训练LLM复现该硬标签，然而人类行为本质上是主观的，同一人在同一情境下可能合理做出不同行为，观察到的响应只是潜在响应分布中的一个抽样，导致准确率评估不可靠、硬标签训练具有误导性。

Method: 1) 提出主观性系数（subjectivity coefficient），一种基于熵的度量，用于区分客观任务（如编码）与主观任务（如社会仿真），并系统分析准确率评估和硬标签训练随主观性增大而失效的机理。2) 提出SALT（Subjectivity-Adaptive soft-Label Training）：将语义相近输入中观察到的输出汇聚为软分布标签，聚合半径根据每个输入的主观性估计自适应调整；在近客观极限下邻域收缩，自然退化为标准单标签训练。3) 构建SUBJSIM基准，包含19,300个上下文、193名标注者、100个主观问题，用于分布级评估。

Result: 实验在SUBJSIM上进行，训练模型使用单观测输出（贴近现实数据条件），评估则针对完整响应分布进行，验证了SALT在现实设定下的可行性与优越性，相较传统准确率评估和硬标签训练方法展现出明显优势。

Conclusion: 本文重新审视了LLM社会仿真中人类行为主观性对评估与训练范式的影响，提出了面向主观性的系统性解决方案（主观性系数+SALT+SUBJSIM基准），表明在评估LLM仿真人类行为时应以分布而非单一硬标签为准则，为更可靠的社会仿真提供了新的方法论基础。

Abstract: LLM-based social simulation is a promising complement to traditional methods such as surveys and behavioral experiments. A core question is how to evaluate the fidelity of LLM-simulated human behavior and optimize LLMs toward it. Prevailing practice evaluates by accuracy, checking whether the model selects the single response observed from a human, and trains the LLM to reproduce this hard label. However, human behavior is inherently subjective: the same person in the same situation may reasonably act differently, so an observed response is only one draw from an underlying response distribution, rendering accuracy-based evaluation unreliable and hard-label training misleading. To address these problems, we first introduce the subjectivity coefficient, an entropy-based quantity distinguishing objective tasks such as coding from subjective ones such as social simulation, and use it to systematically analyze how accuracy-based evaluation and hard-label training fail as subjectivity grows. Based on the subjectivity coefficient, we propose Subjectivity-Adaptive soft-Label Training (SALT): it pools observed outputs from semantically nearby inputs into soft distributional labels, with an aggregation radius adapted to the estimated subjectivity of each input; in the near-objective limit the neighborhood shrinks, so SALT naturally falls back to standard single-label training. Moreover, since existing datasets record only single observed responses and cannot support distributional evaluation, we construct SUBJSIM, a benchmark of 19,300 contexts covering 193 annotators and 100 subjective questions.
