Experiments across two model families and five representative memory frameworks show that MemTrapBench is challenging: all evaluated memory strategies underperform the no-memory setting, with even the strongest methods suffering drops of more than 10%. To mitigate these cognitive traps, we propose AdaptiveMem, a simple yet effective inference-time method that instructs LLMs to avoid memory traps. AdaptiveMem mitigates cognitive traps on MemTrapBench while preserving or improving performance on standard memory benchmarks across diverse memory frameworks.

</details>


### [143] [ContractScrub: A benchmark for final review of legal contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20204)
*Yejin Bang,Kirsty Fielding,Brandan Oliver,Brian Birke,Nabeel Seedat,Andrew M. Bean*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ContractScrub，这是首个专门用于评估LLM合同审查（contract scrubbing）能力的基准测试，包含由资深律师手工编写的合同，覆盖多种错误类型（如定义术语误用、错误引用、语言不一致等）。实验发现前沿模型在该任务上表现令人惊讶地差，仅有一个模型达到0.75的宏平均召回率，尽管这些模型在看似相关的一般基准上表现强劲，揭示了当前模型的实际局限以及构建领域特定基准的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合同审查（scrubbing）是法律工作中的一个重要任务，涉及对交易协议进行最终错误和不一致性的检查。由于该任务对长文本推理、一致性检查和命名实体识别能力的要求与LLM现有能力高度契合，且具有巨大的经济价值和自动化潜力，但此前尚无任何对LLM执行该任务的正式评估。因此需要建立专门的基准来测量实际能力。

Method: 创建ContractScrub基准，由经验丰富的律师手工制作合同，涵盖多种错误类别，包括定义术语的误用、不正确的引用以及不一致的语言等。基于该基准对前沿模型进行系统性测试，评估其合同审查能力，并与一般基准上的表现进行对比。

Result: 前沿模型在合同审查任务上表现不佳，仅有一个模型达到0.75的宏平均召回率。尽管这些模型在看似相关的一般基准指标上表现强劲，但在这种针对性的领域特定任务上暴露出明显的性能差距。

Conclusion: 当前前沿LLM在合同审查这一高价值法律任务上仍存在实际局限，凸显了领域特定、针对性强的基准对于衡量模型真实世界影响的重要性，同时也为未来在该方向上的模型改进提供了参照起点。

Abstract: Legal work, with its heavy reliance on processing large amounts of text, is often considered one of the domains most exposed to the use of LLMs. Contract ``scrubbing,'' the final review of transactional agreements for errors and inconsistencies, is a particularly suitable task for automation, because it is routine, painstaking work requiring detailed attention to long documents. Scrubbing also seems to align naturally with the general capabilities expected of frontier LLMs around long-context reasoning, consistency checking, and named entity recognition (NER). Despite the economic value and potential for automation, no formal evaluations of LLMs performing contract scrubbing have been conducted. We introduce ContractScrub, the first benchmark designed to evaluate contract scrubbing capabilities, comprising contracts hand-crafted by experienced lawyers over diverse error categories such as misuse of defined terms, incorrect references, and inconsistent language. Frontier models perform surprisingly poorly with only one model reaching 0.75 macro average recall despite strong performance on seemingly related general benchmarks, demonstrating the practical limits of current models and the importance of narrowly targeted, domain-specific benchmarks for measuring real-world impact.

</details>


### [144] [Electronic Navigational Chart Change Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20218)
*Jacob Arndt,Abhishek Potnis,Alexandre Sorokine*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化分类电子航海图（ENC）变更的方法，通过将复杂的矢量数据变更编码为结构化表格格式，结合空间上下文编码器和ENC属性编码器，使用梯度提升树在两个运营数据集上分别达到90%和94%的准确率，证明了机器学习在运营地理空间管道中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水文办公室面临的核心挑战是判断海图变更是对航海安全构成关键还是非关键风险。现有工作流高度依赖人工审查和验证，存在劳动强度大、扩展性差（难以应对海量海图更新）以及分析师之间判断不一致等问题，亟需自动化解决方案。

Method: 提出了一个自动化ENC变更分类方法：首先建立基线编码方案，将复杂矢量数据变更转化为适合分类模型的结构化表格格式。编码方案包含两个关键组件：(1) 空间上下文编码器，用于丰富变更表示与周围地理特征的关联；(2) ENC属性编码器，用于表示修改对象的细微属性值描述。最终使用梯度提升树模型进行分类。

Result: 在两个不同的运营数据集（共1,308个海图对、超过10万个单独海图修改）上进行评估。调整超参数后的梯度提升树在两个数据集上分别达到90%和94%的准确率，相比未使用空间上下文和属性嵌入的默认超参数模型提升了5-7%。

Conclusion: 结果表明将机器学习整合到运营地理空间管道中以改善ENC维护和增强航海安全是可行的。实验还证明简单的定位和空间聚合方法已能展现有效性，为未来在该应用中评估更复杂的空间表示学习技术奠定了基础。

Abstract: Electronic Navigational Charts (ENCs) are geospatial vector datasets used in maritime navigation systems that represent hydrographic and navigational information such as depths, navigational aids, traffic schemes, and hazards. A major challenge for hydrographic offices is determining whether a given chart change poses a critical or non-critical risk to maritime safety. Existing workflows rely heavily on manual review and verification, which is labor-intensive, scales poorly with the volume of incoming chart updates, and introduces inter-analyst inconsistencies. To address this challenge, we propose a method for automated classification of ENC changes. We establish a baseline encoding scheme to translate complex vector data changes into a structured tabular format for classification models. The two crucial components of the encoding scheme include a spatial context encoder to enrich the change representations with surrounding geographic features, and an ENC attribute encoder to represent nuanced attribute-value descriptions of the modified objects. We evaluate the proposed approach across two distinct operational datasets, comprising 1,308 chart pairs containing over 100,000 individual chart modifications. Tuned gradient-boosted trees leveraging the proposed encoding schemes achieve accuracies of 90% and 94% on the two datasets, yielding a 5-7% improvement over default hyperparameterized models trained on encodings without spatial context and attribute embeddings. These results demonstrate the viability of integrating machine learning into operational geospatial pipelines to improve ENC maintenance and enhance maritime safety. Finally, our experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of simple location and spatial aggregation methods, providing a foundation for evaluating more sophisticated spatial representation learning techniques for this application.

</details>


### [145] [InsufficiencyBench: Evaluating LLM legal advice on underspecified user queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20220)
*Samuel J. Vincent,Daniel Calloway,Fangyi Yu,Andrew M. Bean,Nabeel Seedat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出InsufficiencyBench，首个针对法律查询端信息缺失的法律基准，评估模型能否识别查询中缺失的法定关键信息、指出缺失内容并避免过早结论。基于八类缺失元素分类体系和三种结构性失败模式，构建202个基准项，评估十个前沿模型后发现最优F2仅为0.46，中位召回0.44，模型要么无差别地含糊表述，要么在虚构假设下静默作答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律AI基准假设查询信息完整充分，而实际用户常遗漏对法律结果具有实质性影响的事实。这种查询端信息不足（query-side insufficiency）会导致模型基于不完整信息给出错误法律结论，但此前缺乏针对该问题的系统性评估基准。

Method: 形式化了八类标准缺失元素分类体系，涵盖三种结构性失败模式：switch（切换）、gating（门控）和fatal prerequisite（致命前提）。构建202个基准项（58个基础查询，144个缺陷变体），覆盖六个法律领域和24个美国司法辖区，由执业律师标注。评估十个前沿模型。

Result: 所有模型在缺失元素识别上F2最高仅0.46，中位召回0.44。模型要么无差别地规避式作答，要么在臆造的前提下静默给出答案。没有任何模型既能对缺陷查询进行识别和限定回答，又能直接回答完整性查询。

Conclusion: 当前前沿法律AI系统尚不具备可靠检测查询端法律信息缺失的能力，且存在过度规避与过度自信的两难。未来需要开发能同时兼顾完整查询直接回答和缺陷查询谨慎处理的模型，该基准为此提供了可复现的评估框架。

Abstract: Legal AI systems are increasingly used to answer legal questions, yet existing benchmarks assume queries arrive fully specified. In practice, users omit facts that materially determine the legal outcome. We introduce InsufficiencyBench, the first legal benchmark targeting query-side insufficiency: whether a model recognizes when a query lacks legally material information, identifies what is missing, and refrains from premature conclusions. We formalize a taxonomy of eight canonical missing-element categories across three structural failure modes---switch, gating, and fatal prerequisite--- and construct 202 benchmark items (58 base queries, 144 deficient variants) spanning six legal domains and 24 US jurisdictions and annotated by practising attorneys. Evaluating ten frontier models, we find that no model exceeds F2 = 0.46 on missing-element identification and that the median recall is 0.44. Models either hedge indiscriminately or answer silently under fabricated presumptions. No model both identifies and qualifies responses to deficient queries while directly addressing complete ones.

</details>


### [146] [Rule-Compliant Visual Spatial Planning for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20237)
*Yu Chen,Ting Lei,Yaoyi Li,Jia Cai,Zhecen Wu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 RuleMaze 基准与 DMP 方法，用于评估和提升多模态大语言模型在显式及未见规则约束下的视觉空间规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs 具备语言推理与视觉感知能力，但其在显式或未见自然语言规则约束下的视觉空间规划能力尚未被充分探索。该场景要求模型联合理解空间布局、解析自然语言规则并据此规划合法动作，现有研究缺乏对此能力的系统评估与方法支撑。

Method: 1) 提出 RuleMaze 可控基准，让 MLLMs 在服从不同复杂度自然语言规则的前提下导航迷宫；2) 提出 Language-Logic-Function Hybridization（语言-逻辑-函数混合化）框架，自动生成自然语言规则并将其转化为逻辑表示与可执行校验器，免去人工规则工程；3) 提出 Disentangled Multimodal Planning (DMP) 方法，将感知、执行与规则验证解耦为可解释的推理原语，以提升规则遵循能力与系统化泛化。

Result: 实验表明，DMP 相比端到端文本规划基线在规则合规性与规划成功率上均有显著提升，且能系统性泛化到更复杂及未见过的规则，并提供透明的中间规划轨迹。

Conclusion: RuleMaze 为多模态大语言模型在接地、可解释的规则基空间规划研究建立了原则性基准，DMP 验证了解耦式规划范式在此任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) combine linguistic reasoning with visual perception, yet their ability to perform visual spatial planning under explicit or previously unseen rule constraints remains underexplored. This setting requires models to jointly understand spatial layouts, interpret natural-language rules, and plan valid actions accordingly. To address this gap, we introduce RuleMaze, a controllable benchmark in which MLLMs must navigate mazes while obeying natural-language rules of varying complexity. RuleMaze isolates rule-compliant spatial planning by requiring accurate perception, rule interpretation, and constrained action planning. To enable scalable and systematic rule construction, we propose Language-Logic-Function Hybridization, which automatically generates natural-language rules and translates them into logical representations and executable validators, eliminating manual rule engineering. To improve rule following and generalization, we introduce Disentangled Multimodal Planning (DMP), which separates perception, execution, and rule verification through interpretable reasoning primitives. By disentangling these components, DMP facilitates systematic generalization to more complex and previously unseen rules, while providing transparent intermediate planning traces. Experiments demonstrate that DMP substantially improves rule compliance and planning success compared to end-to-end textual planning baselines. Overall, RuleMaze establishes a principled benchmark for studying grounded and interpretable rule-based spatial planning in MLLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/oceanflowlab/RuleMaze.

</details>


### [147] [QUASAR: A Quantum-Classical Neural Network for SAR Satellite Physical-Layer Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20240)
*Vincenzo Sammartino,Nathanael Denis,Roberto Di Pietro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QUASAR，这是首个将CNN频谱图编码器与变分量子电路（VQC）融合的量子-经典混合架构，用于X波段SAR卫星信号的物理层认证（PLA）。相比经典机器学习基线，QUASAR仅需10%的训练数据即可达到同等精度，在相同数据预算下分类精度更高，且在重放、伪造IQ注入和星载欺骗三种对抗场景下分别以89.7%、94.1%和81.3%的拒真率拒绝欺骗传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X波段SAR卫星在灾害响应、环境监测和军事情报中至关重要，但缺乏稳健的物理层认证（PLA）。现有PLA系统多基于射频指纹，局限于sub-6 GHz频段且依赖经典深度学习，在面对卫星硬件独特的IQ相位非线性特征时存在欠拟合问题，无法为X波段卫星提供有效认证。

Method: QUASAR采用量子-经典混合架构：CNN频谱图编码器提取信号特征，变分量子电路（VQC）处理IQ相位非线性信息。该方案在三种对抗场景（重放攻击、伪造IQ注入、星载欺骗）下进行认证评估，并与经典机器学习基线进行数据效率和分类精度的对比。

Result: QUASAR在三种对抗场景下分别以89.7%、94.1%和81.3%的拒真率拒绝欺骗传输；仅需10%的训练数据即可匹配经典基线的精度；在同等数据预算下分类精度优于经典基线，建立了首个量子增强的卫星星座物理层分类器。

Conclusion: QUASAR验证了量子-经典混合架构在X波段SAR卫星物理层认证中的可行性，展示了量子增强方法在数据效率方面的显著优势，为物理层认证开辟了全新的研究路径。

Abstract: X-band SAR satellites (8-12 GHz) play a critical role in disaster response, environmental monitoring, and military intelligence. Yet, they lack robust physical-layer authentication (PLA), a security layer orthogonal to cryptographic solutions. Existing PLA systems, typically based on radio-frequency fingerprinting, are often limited to sub-6 GHz frequencies and rely on classical deep learning. However, this approach underfits the IQ phase nonlinearities that distinguish satellite hardware. In this paper, we present QUASAR, to the best of our knowledge the first quantum-classical hybrid architecture that fuses a CNN spectrogram encoder with a variational quantum circuit (VQC) to provide PLA to X-band SAR signals. Our solution enjoys two distinctive features: (i) it is markedly more data-efficient than classical machine learning, requiring only 10% of the training data to match the accuracy of classical baselines -- data collection being notoriously the most time-consuming phase of PLA; and, (ii) at an equal data budget, it improves classification accuracy over those baselines. In detail, we test our solution under three adversarial scenarios: replay, crafted-IQ injection, and space-borne spoofing. QUASAR rejects spoofed transmissions in 89.7%, 94.1%, and 81.3% of attempts, respectively, establishing the first quantum-enhanced physical-layer classifier for satellite constellations. The fully detailed framework and the supporting results, other than being interesting on their own, show a novel research avenue for physical-layer authentication.

</details>


### [148] [Learning When to Think: Adaptive Reasoning for Test-Time Compute Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20256)
*Gijs Kassenaar,Zhao Yang,Vincent François-Lavet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出让推理语言模型自适应地分配推理努力：模型在响应首token中自行选择NoThink、Short或Long三种推理模式之一，该选择在GRPO中学习得到，无需独立路由器。在1.5B模型和MATH数据集上，三种模式被有效学习且不坍缩，在维持相近精度的同时将平均响应长度从4,796降至2,811 token（41%减少），并能在GSM8K等基准上以76%的token缩减迁移泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的推理语言模型通常使用固定token预算而非自适应的显式预算，这导致简单问题上过度计算、困难问题上计算不足。核心问题在于模型能否学会自我分配推理努力。

Method: 模型在响应的第一个token处从三种模式中选择：NoThink（快速作答）、Short（简要推理）、Long（延长推理）。该选择在GRPO内部学习，无需单独的router模块。通过构造形状化的奖励函数使每种模式在各自的响应长度区间内有价值，同时配合每种模式硬性token上限来保证模式之间保持区分度。

Result: 在MATH上训练的1.5B蒸馏模型上，三种模式成功涌现且未坍缩为一个选择。简短模式比Long模式更准确，说明router按难度而非随机排序问题。在三个种子上平均，MATH500上精度接近基线（0.782 vs 0.796），平均响应长度从4,796降至2,811 token（41%减少）。无需重训练即可迁移到其他基准，在GSM8K上实现76%的token缩减，且在相近响应长度下精度高于基线。

Conclusion: 研究构建了一个能针对每个问题自适应决定推理量的推理模型，在保持精度的同时显著减少计算开销，且具备跨基准的零样本迁移能力，证明了模型内部可以学习难度感知的推理努力分配。

Abstract: Reasoning language models trained with reinforcement learning typically operate under a fixed token budget rather than an explicitly adaptive one, which can lead to over-computation on easy problems and insufficient computation on difficult ones. We study whether a model can learn to allocate its own reasoning effort by choosing, as the first token of its response, one of three modes: \textsc{NoThink} (answer as quickly as possible), \textsc{Short} (brief reasoning), or \textsc{Long} (extended reasoning). The choice is learned inside Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with no separate router, through a shaped reward that makes each mode worthwhile at a different response length, together with hard per-mode token caps that keep the modes distinct. On a 1.5B distilled model trained on MATH, the three modes emerge without collapsing to a single choice, and the brief modes end up more accurate than \textsc{Long}, which shows that the router sorts problems by difficulty rather than at random. Averaged over three seeds, the resulting policy stays close to the base model's accuracy on the held-out MATH500 ($0.782$ vs.\ $0.796$) while cutting the mean response length from $4{,}796$ to $2{,}811$ tokens (a $41\%$ reduction). Interestingly, it also transfers to other benchmarks without retraining, with the largest savings where problems are easier, with for instance 76\% token reduction on GSM8K and at higher accuracy than the baselines at similar response length. In short, we build a reasoning model that adaptively chooses how much to reason for each problem.

</details>


### [149] [Catching the Rug: Early Prediction of Fraudulent Memecoins on Solana via Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20271)
*Jianghai Li,Pavel Kuznetsov,Yury Yanovich,Konstantin Nott-Whaley,Igor Vodolazov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次在Solana生态系统中开展大规模（640万个代币）memecoin rug pull早期检测研究，发现大多数memecoin在发布一小时内即表现出rug pull特征，并证明仅使用前5分钟交易数据的XGBoost模型即可实现稳健检测，且多源数据融合（PumpFun与Raydium）能有效缓解跨平台域偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链平台上memecoin的快速扩散加剧了rug pull欺诈风险，但此前研究主要聚焦于Ethereum上的代币（利用智能合约后门），而Solana作为按交易量和代币数量领先的memecoin区块链，其rug pull主要由流动性和社会动态驱动，尚缺乏系统的早期检测研究。

Method: 构建了涵盖7个月、640万个代币的Solana生态大规模数据集；采用经典机器学习模型（尤其是XGBoost）利用交易数据前5分钟内的特征进行rug pull检测；并评估了PumpFun与Raydium之间的跨平台泛化能力，采用多源数据融合策略缓解域偏移。

Result: 绝大多数memecoin在发布一小时内即表现出rug pull特征；在没有代码级特征的情况下，XGBoost仅基于前5分钟交易数据即可实现稳健的检测性能；多源数据融合显著提升了跨平台检测的可靠性。

Conclusion: 本研究推进了对高吞吐量链上DeFi欺诈的理解，为投资者提供了实用的rug pull早期检测框架，填补了Solana生态系统中此领域的研究空白。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of memecoins on blockchain platforms has increased the risk of fraudulent activities, particularly rug pulls. While previous studies have focused on Ethereum-based tokens, this paper shifts the spotlight to Solana, the leading blockchain for memecoins by trading volume and token count. Unlike Ethereum, where rug pulls often exploit smart contract backdoors, Solana memecoin rug pulls are predominantly driven by liquidity manipulation and social dynamics. This research pioneers large-scale rug pull early detection in the Solana ecosystem by assembling a dataset of 6.4 million tokens over 7 months. Market analysis reveals that a vast majority of these memecoins exhibit rug pull characteristics within one hour of launch, highlighting the urgency of short-horizon prediction. Despite the absence of code-level features, we demonstrate that classic machine learning models, particularly Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), achieve robust performance in detecting potential rug pulls using only the first 5 minutes of trading data. Furthermore, we evaluate cross-platform generalization between PumpFun and Raydium, revealing that multi-source data fusion significantly mitigates domain shift and improves detection reliability. This study advances the understanding of DeFi fraud on high-throughput chains and provides a practical framework for protecting investors.

</details>


### [150] [Break It Down, Pass It On: Cross-Task Skill Transfer in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20274)
*Yiyang Feng,Biddut Sarker Bijoy,Niranjan Balasubramanian,Jiawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对LLM智能体的技能归纳方式如何影响技能跨任务迁移进行了全面受控研究，发现子任务级技能归纳和文本格式技能迁移效果更好，并提出一个仅需技能与任务描述即可计算的技能效用分数来预测迁移成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体可从已完成任务中归纳技能并复用，但实际中归纳的技能可能迁移不可靠甚至损害智能体性能。技能在何种条件下能可靠跨任务迁移仍是一个未解问题，需要系统性研究技能归纳方式与迁移效果的关系。

Method: 进行了受控的全面比较研究，沿两个维度考察技能归纳方法：(1) 任务级与子任务级技能归纳；(2) 文本与代码技能格式。同时提出两个互补的技能属性——specificity（技能与真实任务的匹配度）和abstractness（技能相关性跨任务的分布均匀度），并将二者结合为技能效用分数，该分数仅需技能和任务描述即可计算，无需任务执行。

Result: 任务级技能大多使智能体性能低于无记忆基线，而子任务级技能平均可提升性能；文本技能比代码技能迁移效果更好。单独来看specificity和abstractness都无法预测任务成功，但二者结合（技能效用分数）与技能迁移时的任务成功一致相关，且子任务级和文本技能具有更高分数。

Conclusion: 技能迁移的可靠性取决于技能归纳方式：子任务级归纳和文本格式更优。技能效用分数作为无需任务执行的实用诊断工具，可在新任务运行前评估技能记忆的质量，为LLM智能体技能系统的设计提供指导。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can induce skills from completed tasks and reuse them later to grow more capable with experience. In practice, induced skills may transfer unreliably and can even harm the agent that retrieves them. When agent-induced skills transfer reliably across tasks remains an open question. We conduct a comprehensive and controlled study of how the way skills are induced shapes their transfer across tasks. Specifically, we compare task-level with subtask-level skill induction and text with code skill formats, the two axes along which existing methods differ. Task-level skills mostly reduce the agent's performance below its no-memory baseline while subtask-level skills raise it above on average, and text skills transfer better than code skills. To further understand our findings, we examine two complementary properties of the induced skills: specificity, which measures how closely a skill matches real tasks, and abstractness, which measures how evenly its relevance spreads across tasks. Neither property alone predicts task success, but their combined effect does, which we propose as a skill utility score. The score correlates consistently with task success when skills are transferred, and subtask-level and text skills score higher. Computing skill utility only needs the skills and task descriptions but not any task execution, so our score serves as a practical diagnostic of a skill memory before any new task runs.

</details>


### [151] [Phantom Gains: Auditing Self-Improvement Against a Measured Null](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20290)
*Cheng Xu,Nan Yan,Liming Chen,M-Tahar Kechadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文识别了LoRA自训练评估中的七种测量缺陷，这些缺陷在缺乏冻结对照组时会反转报告的结论，并提出一种基于逐问题精确检验、在FDR控制下的审计方法，该方法无需新增实验即可构建零分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的改进不再仅以平均准确率衡量，而是以个别问题的得失来评判。由于对两次噪声估计做差分，这种过渡追踪极易受测量伪影干扰，导致虚假的能力变化结论。

Method: 作者对Qwen3-8B进行三轮秩32的LoRA自训练，与通过完全相同流程的冻结对照组比较，识别出七种会反转发现结论的测量失败。提出逐问题精确检验（per-problem exact test）替代自然阈值修复，在FDR控制下对池化基线进行检验，并验证其对多重检验规则、错误率和池大小不敏感。同时对蒸馏与三种自训练进行阶梯式实验设计对比。

Result: 研究发现：单次贪婪解码构建的台账在未训练模型上就能制造能力变化（主要源于推理批处理伪影）；扩展统计量给未训练模型赋予0.280的变化率；自然阈值修复无法通过复制验证。新提出的精确检验在隔离复现上未检测到任何信号。审计发现外部蒸馏能改善基础模型很少触及的问题，而三种自训练不能；回归检验（p<10^-8）表明该不对称性不是蒸馏总体增益更大的副产品。自训练在基线已解决问题上的损坏率远高于测量底噪。

Conclusion: 转换级审计要求对每一个报告的统计量都配备独立测量的零分布。这些零分布无需新增实验，可从多臂研究已有的基线复现中构建，但需注意样本量至少要达到大多数研究所有的数目之上。

Abstract: Whether a language model has improved itself is increasingly judged not by mean accuracy but by which individual problems it gains and loses. Tracking these transitions means differencing two noisy estimates, leaving them vulnerable to measurement artifacts. Auditing three rounds of rank-$32$ LoRA self-training on Qwen3-8B against a frozen control pushed through the identical pipeline, we identify seven measurement failures, each of which inverts a reported finding when its control is absent. Several are standard practice. A ledger built on a single greedy decode manufactures capability changes on an untrained model, largely an artifact of inference batching; the expansion statistic separating acquisition from sharpening assigns that same model a rate of $0.280$. The natural threshold repair does not survive replication: estimated across the frozen comparisons such a design already contains, its null stays non-zero. We replace it with a per-problem exact test against a pooled baseline under false-discovery-rate control, which detects nothing on any held-out replicate and is unchanged under the multiple-testing rule, error rate and pool size. Applied to a ladder of arms matched in stream, volume and evaluation, the audit finds that external distillation improves problems the base model rarely reaches while three forms of self-training do not; a regression rejects this asymmetry as a by-product of distillation's larger overall gain ($p < 10^{-8}$). On the far smaller set of problems the base model never reaches, the evidence is inconclusive, while self-training corrupts problems solved at baseline at rates well above the measured floor. Transition-level auditing therefore requires a separately measured null for every statistic it reports: nulls that cost no new experiments, built from baseline replicates a multi-arm study already owns, though not from as few as most possess.

</details>


### [152] [MidTool: Mid-training Data Synthesis for Agentic Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20314)
*Fengqing Jiang,Yite Wang,Boyi Liu,Zhaoyang Wang,Canwen Xu,Zhewei Yao,Radha Poovendran,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MidTool，一个面向智能体工具使用的开放语料构建流水线，通过大规模网页、PDF和代码数据结合真实工具API、MCP技能及文档驱动工作流的合成监督信号进行中期训练（mid-training），在Qwen3-4B/8B-Base模型上验证了该方法在BFCL、tau2-Bench和MCP Universe基准上的显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已有研究证明中期训练可以增强数学、科学等推理密集型能力以及软件工程场景中的代理能力，但通用工具使用这一同等重要的代理能力尚未得到系统探索。作者希望验证通用工具使用是否也应像其他重要LLM能力一样受益于专用的中期训练阶段。

Method: 提出MidTool流水线：(1) 构建MidTool-Mix语料，融合大规模网页、PDF和代码数据；(2) 利用真实世界工具API、MCP（模型上下文协议）技能和文档驱动工作流生成合成监督信号；(3) 在Qwen3-4B-Base和Qwen3-8B-Base上进行中期训练；(4) 后续采用监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）两阶段的后续后训练。

Result: 在SFT和RL两种后训练设置下，MidTool-Mix在BFCL、tau2-Bench和MCP Universe三个基准上均持续优于基线模型，证明了该中期训练语料的有效性。

Conclusion: 通用工具使用能力与其他重要LLM能力一样，应通过专门的中期训练来强化，而非完全留给后期后训练阶段。MidTool为这一方向提供了开放的语料构建方案和实证支持。

Abstract: Mid-training is increasingly recognized as a critical stage for shaping the capabilities of large language models. Recent work has shown that targeted mid-training can strengthen reasoning-intensive abilities such as math and science, and can also improve agentic capabilities in software-engineering settings. In this work, we study the parallel but less explored agentic capability: general tool use. We present MidTool, an open corpus construction pipeline for agentic tool-use mid-training that combines large-scale web, PDF, and code data with synthesized supervision from real-world tool APIs, MCP skills, and document-grounded workflows. MidTool is designed to teach models how to recognize tool affordances, ground arguments from context, compose tool call workflow, and recover from incomplete information. We mid-train Qwen3-4B-Base and Qwen3-8B-Base on MidTool-Mix, and then apply follow-up post-training with both supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Compared with baselines, MidTool-Mix consistently improves downstream performance under both SFT and RL on BFCL, tau2-Bench, and MCP Universe. These results suggest that general tool use, like other important LLM capabilities, benefits from dedicated mid-training rather than being left entirely to post-training.

</details>


### [153] [Pandora's AI Model Routing Box: Efficient Allocation with Costly Value Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20316)
*Adam Fisch,Shubhendu Trivedi,Fantine Huot,William W. Cohen,Michael Kaisers,Mirella Lapata,Kate Larson,Jacob Eisenstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Pandora's Router和Pandora's Bidder两种异构AI系统路由策略，将"估计每个专家模型价值回报的成本-收益权衡"形式化为Pandora's Box问题，在Gaussian信号模型下推导出闭式信息价值表达式，决定何时值得为每个输入细化价值估计。实验表明路由质量与穷尽估计相当，但极大减少了昂贵估计器的调用次数；在去中心化场景中，信息价值推理在竞争估计准确时提升分配效率，但估计噪声大时可能损害他人利益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构AI系统（多模型、多架构、多种推理设置）可通过路由查询至最有效且成本最低的专家来提升质量与效率。但路由需要估计每个专家的期望回报，而价值估计本身有成本：廉价估计器快但噪声大，昂贵估计器准但开销高。如何在这个成本-收益权衡下做出最优路由决策是核心问题。

Method: 将价值估计的权衡问题形式化为Pandora's Box（带代价检查的最优搜索经典问题）。在Gaussian信号模型假设下，推导出闭式信息价值表达式，用于决定对每个专家和输入是否值得细化价值估计。提出集中式策略Pandora's Router；并扩展出去中心化策略Pandora's Bidder，其中专家独立决定是否投资自评估，再接受报价认领查询。

Result: 在三个领域（多LLM基准、检索增强专家、变推理时间LLM）的实验中，Pandora's Router的路由质量与穷尽估计相当，但调用昂贵估计器的频率大幅降低。在去中心化场景中，当竞争估计准确时信息价值推理提升分配效率；但当竞争估计噪声大时，会提升策略型专家的效用而损害其他参与者。

Conclusion: 信息价值推理为异构AI系统的路由提供了原则性的成本-收益分析框架，在保持路由质量的同时显著降低估计开销。去中心化场景下需要警惕噪声估计情境中的战略博弈行为对整体分配效率的潜在负面影响。

Abstract: Heterogeneous AI systems composed of multiple models, architectures, harnesses, or inference-time settings can improve quality and efficiency by routing queries to the specialist who can answer most effectively at the lowest cost. Routing requires estimating each specialist's expected return, but this value estimation has a cost. Cheap estimators (e.g., embedding-based predictors) are fast but noisy, while accurate estimators (e.g., fine-tuned models with access to retrieval results or partial reasoning traces) are expensive. We formalize this tradeoff as an instance of Pandora's Box, the classical problem of optimal search with costly inspection. Under a Gaussian signal model, the resulting policies have closed-form value-of-information expressions that determine, for each specialist and input, whether refining the value estimate is worth its cost. We call the centralized policy Pandora's Router. We extend this to a decentralized setting, Pandora's Bidder, where specialists independently decide whether to invest in self-assessment before accepting an offered price to claim a query. Experiments across three domains---a standard multi-LLM benchmark, retrieval-augmented specialists, and LLMs with variable inference-time reasoning---show that Pandora's Router matches the routing quality of exhaustive estimation, while querying the expensive estimator far less often. In the decentralized setting, value-of-information reasoning improves allocative efficiency when competing estimates are accurate; when competing estimates are noisy, however, it can increase the strategic specialist's utility at the expense of others.

</details>


### [154] [AI4AI-Bench: Benchmarking LLM Agents in Algorithmic Design for Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20318)
*Yizhe Chi,Wenyi Li,Deyao Hong,Xiaoqiu Wang,Mingju Gao,Kaisen Yang,Bingxiang He,Youjie Zheng,Calvin Xiao,Qinhuai Na*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI4AI-Bench，一个包含10个冻结研究仓库、覆盖10类训练算法家族的基准测试，用于衡量AI系统能否设计训练算法（即递归自我改进的技术核心）。实验表明当前最强AI系统平均得分仅0.166，即使最优配置也仅达到0.250，无法有效改进训练算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进（RSI）的核心在于AI系统能否设计更好的训练算法，但现有基准要么比拼数据收集、要么比拼超参数调优，没有任何基准能纯粹衡量"改变训练算法"这一能力。因此需要一个专门的基准来隔离和衡量这种能力。

Method: 构建AI4AI-Bench：包含10个冻结的研究仓库，覆盖10类训练算法家族（如目标函数、更新规则等）。每个任务中，智能体有4小时（在B300 GPU上）重写训练算法，随后从头重跑最多12小时，由隐藏的固定评估器打分，并与仓库原算法在相同流程下对比。10个任务使用统一的0-1量表映射：0为无信息模型，0.1为仓库自带算法，1.0为任务最优值。

Result: 29种系统配置（6个系统×10个任务）的均分为0.166，最强系统达0.250，即最优系统也只完成仓库算法与最优值之间差距的五分之一以下。分析发现多数提交完全未改变模型学习方式；少数真正改变算法的提交均分0.226 vs 未改变的0.126。增加推理预算能将真正修改算法的提交比例从8%提升到64%，均分从0.094提升到0.196。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统在"设计训练算法"这一RSI核心能力上表现甚弱，离实现递归自我改进仍有很大距离。推理预算增加更主要带来的是"愿意去修改"的意愿而非修改质量。作者公开全部任务套件、评估器和提交记录，以便随系统进步持续复测。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) asks whether an AI system can improve the process that produces AI systems, so that the next system inherits the improvement. That process is the training algorithm: a better objective or update rule improves the compute\mbox{-}capability exchange rate for every subsequent run, including the one that produces the next agent. Whether RSI is feasible therefore turns on whether an agent can design training algorithms. No benchmark isolates that ability: existing suites are won by collecting data or by tuning hyperparameters, and none tells a change to how a run is executed apart from a change to how the model learns. We present AI4AI\mbox{-}Bench, 10 frozen research repositories spanning 10 training algorithm families. In each task, an agent has 4 hours on one B300 to rewrite the training algorithm; its code is then rerun from scratch for up to 12 hours and scored by a fixed evaluator hidden from the agent, against the repository's original algorithm under the same procedure. Because the 10 metrics are incommensurable, every task is mapped onto one scale on which $0$ is an uninformative model, $0.1$ is the algorithm the repository ships, and $1.0$ is the task optimum. Across 29 configurations of 6 systems on all 10 tasks the mean score is $0.166$, and the best system reaches $0.250$: even the strongest closes under a fifth of the distance between the algorithm that was already there and the optimum. The submissions show where that distance went: most never change how the model learns at all, and the minority that do average $0.226$ against $0.126$ for the rest. More reasoning effort mostly buys the willingness to go there, taking that minority from $8\%$ of submissions to $64\%$ and the mean score from $0.094$ to $0.196$. We release the task suite, the evaluators and every scored submission, so that the measurement can be repeated as these systems change.

</details>


### [155] [An Agentic Approach for Active Data Collection, Travel Behavior Modeling, and Weather-Sensitive Demand Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20320)
*Narges Ahmadi,Yubo Jiao,Jônatas Augusto Manzolli,Jiangbo Yu,Luis Miranda-Moreno*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三智能体工作流，将对话式数据采集、结构化数据处理和行为预测相结合。通过聊天机器人管理的图像增强陈述偏好调查收集了454个受访者-场景观测数据，比较了多项Logit模型、随机森林和九种本地部署的大语言模型（2-350亿参数）。随机森林达到69.6%五分类准确率，最佳文本零样本LLM达69.9%，而结合天气图像的视觉配置达到71.5%，表明视觉上下文可为特定模型提供额外预测信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 出行行为研究中，数字数据采集和预测建模两个阶段通常被分开开发和评估，缺乏端到端的协调工作流。该研究旨在弥合数据采集（对话式调查）与预测建模（传统统计模型、机器学习和LLM）之间的割裂，探索多智能体协同的整合框架。

Method: 构建三智能体工作流：（1）聊天机器人管理的图像增强陈述偏好调查用于数据采集；（2）结构化数据处理；（3）行为预测。分析采用多项Logit模型与逻辑回归、随机森林作为基线，同时评估9种本地部署的LLM（2-350亿参数），覆盖四种零样本提示条件，并扩展测试了人物角色、少样本和视觉配置。

Result: 随机森林五分类准确率为69.6%，最佳文本零样本LLM达69.9%且无需任务专用拟合。习惯性出行信息带来最一致的性能提升，专家框架总体优于角色扮演，人物角色信息在缺少习惯性出行信息时最有用。少样本提示对多种模型有效且收益在小样本量后趋于稳定。结合受访者所见天气图像的最佳视觉配置达到71.5%五分类准确率。

Conclusion: 该研究证明对话式调查、结构化数据处理、传统行为建模、机器学习和多模态LLM预测可以在一个可审计的多智能体工作流中协调运作，为出行行为研究的端到端集成提供了可行范式，同时验证了视觉上下文对特定LLM预测性能的增益作用。

Abstract: Travel behavior research increasingly combines digital data collection with predictive modeling, yet these stages are often developed and evaluated separately. This study proposes a three-agent workflow integrating conversational data collection, structured data processing, and behavioral prediction. A chatbot-administered, image-augmented stated-preference survey collected mode choices from student commuters across five predefined weather scenarios, yielding 454 respondent-scenario observations. Weather-related associations were analyzed using a multinomial logit model, while logistic regression and random forest provided machine-learning benchmarks. Nine locally deployed large language models (LLMs), ranging from 2 to 35 billion parameters, were evaluated across four zero-shot prompt-and-context conditions and extended through persona, few-shot, and vision-based configurations. Random forest achieved 69.6% five-class accuracy, while the best text-only zero-shot LLM reached 69.9% without task-specific fitting. Habitual travel information produced the most consistent gains, Expert framing generally outperformed Role-Play, and persona information was most useful when habitual travel information was unavailable. Few-shot prompting improved prediction for several models, with gains stabilizing after a small number of examples. Using the same weather images shown to respondents, the best vision-based configuration reached 71.5% five-class accuracy, indicating that visual context may provide additional predictive information for selected models. Overall, the study shows how conversational surveys, structured data processing, conventional behavioral modeling, machine learning, and multimodal LLM prediction can be coordinated within an auditable multi-agent workflow.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

### [156] [Deep Learning for Automated Quantification of Tumor-Associated Macrophages from H&E-Stained Slides in Diffuse Large B-Cell Lymphoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19283)
*Anastasiia Studenikina,Svetlana Illarionova,Joaquim Carreras,Daniil Sulimov,Olga Filimonova,Dmitry Zvezdin,Arsenii Galimov,Ivan Tyukin,Rifat Hamoudi,Maxim Sharaev*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本研究探索使用深度学习直接从HE染色组织切片中量化M2型肿瘤相关巨噬细胞（TAM），以替代资源密集型的CD163免疫组化（IHC）检测。基于52例DLBCL患者、1,713个TAM实例的数据集，评估了五种深度学习架构，其中Swin-U-Net表现出最佳分割性能（F1=0.633），且其预测的CD163水平与IHC金标准接近（20.8%截断值），可作为DLBCL中TAM预后评估的可扩展、成本效益高的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: M2型肿瘤相关巨噬细胞（TAM）已被确认为DLBCL疾病侵袭性的指标，但传统的CD163免疫组化（IHC）检测资源密集、成本高昂且耗时。研究者希望通过深度学习直接对常规HE染色切片进行TAM量化，以实现更具可扩展性、成本效益更高的预后评估方案。

Method: 使用52例DLBCL患者的配准数据集（高分辨率HE图像与IHC验证的1,713个TAM实例标注），评估五种深度学习架构：U-Net、Swin-U-Net、Cerberus-U-Net3+、YOLOv11和HoVer-Net。进行CD163 TAM密度的生存分析（Kaplan-Meier和多元Cox回归），并将各模型的预测结果与IHC金标准进行对照验证。

Result: 高CD163 TAM密度（>20.04%）显著关联更差的总生存（HR 2.73; p=0.012）和无进展生存（HR 2.88; p=0.011）。Cerberus-U-Net3+获得最高灵敏度（Recall 0.656），Swin-U-Net获得最佳分割保真度（Precision 0.694, F1=0.633）。Swin-U-Net预测的CD163水平截断值为20.8%，与IHC接近，且高预测值患者总生存呈下降趋势（HR 2.63; p=0.083）。

Conclusion: 采用移动窗口自注意力机制的深度学习架构（Swin-U-Net）有潜力成为IHC的候选替代方案，实现DLBCL中TAM预后评估的可扩展性和成本效益优化，为临床病理诊断提供了无需免疫染色的数字化解决方案。

Abstract: While M2-polarized tumor-associated macrophages (TAMs) have been established as indicators of disease aggressiveness in diffuse large B-cell lymphoma (DLBCL), traditional CD163 immunohistochemistry (IHC) remains resource-intensive. This study aims to investigate the feasibility of using deep learning to quantify TAMs directly from standard hematoxylin and eosin-stained (HE) tissue sections. Using a curated dataset of 52 patients with DLBCL, with high-resolution HE images and IHC-validated annotations (1,713 TAM instances), five architectures were evaluated: U-Net, Swin-U-Net, Cerberus-U-Net3+, YOLOv11, and HoVer-Net. High CD163 TAM density (>20.04%) was associated with significantly reduced overall survival (HR 2.73; 95% CI:1.21-6.16; p=0.012) and progression-free survival (HR 2.88; 95% CI: 1.23-6.76; p=0.011). In multivariate Cox proportional hazards analysis adjusting for IPI, molecular subtype (GCB/non-GCB per Hans algorithm), EBV status, and age, CD163 TAM density showed a prognostic trend for overall survival (HR 3.24; 95% CI: 0.94-11.15; p=0.062) and progression-free survival (HR 2.41; 95% CI: 0.80-7.32; p=0.120). Among the evaluated models, the domain-specific Cerberus-U-Net3+ achieved the highest sensitivity (Recall 0.656), while the Transformer-based Swin-U-Net demonstrated superior segmentation fidelity (Precision 0.694, F1-score 0.633). Additionally, the survival analysis based on the predicted Swin-U-Net CD163 level revealed a 20.8% cutoff point for patients with high and low CD163 levels near IHC, as well as a downward trend in overall survival among patients with higher predicted CD163 values of 2.63 (95% CI: 0.85-8.33; p=0.083). These findings suggest that deep learning architectures using shifted-window self-attention show potential as a candidate surrogate for IHC that is scalable and cost-effective for prognostic assessment of TAMs in DLBCL.

</details>


### [157] [MOSAIC: A Self-supervised Dynamic Multi-encoding Reconstruction Framework for 3D Late Gadolinium Enhancement MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19506)
*Muhammad A. Sultan,Yingmin Liu,Katherine Binzel,Katarzyna E. Gil,Karolina M. Zareba,Rizwan Ahmad*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种名为MOSAIC的自监督动态重建框架，用于高度欠采样的双回波三维钆延迟增强（3D LGE）MRI。该框架直接从欠采样数据中联合建模多回波图像内容、线圈灵敏度图和每次心跳特异的非刚性运动，无需全采样训练数据或预计算的灵敏度图。实验结果表明，MOSAIC在数字体模、动物和人体实验中均优于现有方法，能在超过1000倍的加速因子下实现自由呼吸、运动分辨的双回波3D LGE成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D LGE MRI重建方法通常需要将采集数据分箱为不同运动状态，且依赖全采样训练数据集或准确的预计算灵敏度图，这些限制导致高度欠采样情况下的重建质量不佳。作者旨在开发一种无需这些先验条件的自监督重建框架，以实现在极高加速因子下的高质量运动分辨3D LGE成像。

Method: MOSAIC采用自监督方式，从采集到的欠采样数据中联合建模三个关键成分：多回波图像内容、线圈灵敏度图以及每次心跳特异的非刚性运动。与已有方法将数据分箱到不同运动状态（无论有无运动补偿）不同，MOSAIC直接从每个心跳重建运动分辨的3D LGE图像。评估采用数字体模（含模拟心肌疤痕）以及体内动物和人体研究。

Result: 在体模实验中，MOSAIC相比低秩深度图像先验重建及其消融变体，实现了更高的峰值信噪比（PSNR）和结构相似性指数（SSIM）。在动物和人体研究中，MOSAIC获得了比在线图像导航压缩感知和低秩深度图像先验重建更高的盲法专家图像质量评分。

Conclusion: MOSAIC证明了在超过1000倍加速因子下实现运动分辨自由呼吸双回波3D LGE MRI的可行性，相比现有最先进方法具有更好的细节保留和伪影抑制能力。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop and evaluate a self-supervised dynamic reconstruction framework for highly undersampled dual-echo three-dimensional late gadolinium enhancement (3D LGE) MRI.
  Methods: MOSAIC jointly models multi-echo image content, coil sensitivity maps, and beat-specific nonrigid motion directly from acquired undersampled data, without requiring fully sampled training datasets or accurate precomputed sensitivity maps. Unlike existing methods that bin the acquired data into different motion states, with or without motion compensation, MOSAIC reconstructs a motion-resolved 3D LGE image from each heartbeat. The method was evaluated using digital phantoms with simulated myocardial scars and in vivo animal and human studies.
  Results: In phantom experiments, MOSAIC achieved higher peak signal-to-noise ratio and structural similarity index measure than low-rank deep image prior reconstruction and ablation variants of MOSAIC. In animal and human studies, MOSAIC achieved higher blinded expert image-quality scores than inline image-navigated compressed-sensing and low-rank deep image prior reconstructions.
  Conclusion: MOSAIC demonstrated the feasibility of motion-resolved free-breathing dual-echo 3D LGE MRI at acceleration factors exceeding 1,000, with improved detail preservation and artifact suppression relative to the state-of-the-art comparison methods.

</details>


### [158] [Loss-Resilient Semantic Communication over Packet-Loss Networks at Extreme-Low Bandwidth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19590)
*Shengshi Yao,Jincheng Dai,Sixian Wang,Guo Lu,Kai Niu,Wenjun Xu,Wenjun Zhang,Ping Zhang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: ResiGLC是一种针对极端低带宽丢包网络设计的抗丢包生成式潜编码框架，通过利用语言模型的上下文预测能力和掩码学习策略，实现鲁棒的语义通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式语义编解码器仅针对压缩效率优化，缺乏对传输错误的鲁棒性。在极端低带宽网络中，高度紧凑的生成式潜表示对丢包导致的损坏极为敏感，误差在潜上下文和多步解码过程中严重传播，导致保真度和真实感显著下降。

Method: 提出ResiGLC框架：(1)利用生成与压缩之间的目标一致性，充分挖掘语言模型的上下文预测能力；(2)集成掩码学习策略，支持潜码的任意上下文建模，以缓解误差传播并应对不可预测的丢包模式；(3)在接收端设计渐进式抗丢包解码管线，分别利用潜码的上下文关系和生成潜空间中的多模态语义先验；(4)联合优化压缩效率和丢包鲁棒性。

Result: 大量实验表明，在丢包网络条件下，ResiGLC以极端低带宽成本有效提升了感知保真度和真实感方面的抗丢包性能。

Conclusion: ResiGLC通过联合优化压缩效率与丢包鲁棒性，以及渐进式解码机制，在动态丢包场景下提供了优雅的性能退化表现，为极端低带宽语义通信提供了鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: In extreme-low bandwidth network scenarios, generative semantic codecs have emerged as promising solutions to reduce bandwidth cost for visual communications. However, these learned codecs are usually optimized solely for compression efficiency and thus not robust against transmission errors. Corruptions due to packet-loss among these highly compact generative latent representations often cause more critical degradation in fidelity and realism, intensified by the severe error propagation across the latent contexts and multi-step decoding process. In this paper, we propose ResiGLC, a novel loss-resilient generative latent coding framework designed for robust semantic communication over extreme-low bandwidth packet-loss networks. Motivated by the inherent goal-consistency between generation and compression, we sufficiently exploit the impressive in-context predictive capabilities of language models. Integrated with the masked learning strategy, our model supports arbitrary context modeling of latent codes, which could mitigate the error propagation and handle unpredictable packet loss patterns. At the receiver, a progressive resilient decoding pipeline is presented, which leverages both the contextual relationship of the latent codes and the multi-modal semantic prior in the generative latent space, separately. By jointly optimizing toward both compression efficiency and packet-loss resilience, our proposed progressive decoding mechanism offers graceful performance when dealing with dynamic packet losses. Through extensive experimental evaluations, we establish that under packet-loss network conditions, ResiGLC can effectively improve the loss-resilience in terms of perceptual fidelity and realism qualities with extreme-low bandwidth cost.

</details>


### [159] [AsymFeX: A Symmetry-Driven Framework for Ischemic Stroke Segmentation Across Imaging Modalities and Stroke Stages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19769)
*Maunil Shah,Vaanathi Sundaresan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段、兼容nnU-Net的3D分割方法用于急性缺血性卒中（AIS）病灶分割。第一阶段校正头部倾斜以对齐真实解剖中矢状面；第二阶段引入非对称特征提取（AsymFeX）模块，通过跨半球注意力、特征差异估计和双尺度门控，将每个体素与对侧对应体素在3×3×3邻域内比较，有效捕捉大小病灶。在AISD数据集上达到0.6796 Dice、23.53 mm HD95、7.69 mL AVD，显著优于现有SOTA方法，并在ATLAS v2.1和ISLES'24上验证了跨模态、跨时间点的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非对比CT（NCCT）是缺血性梗死诊断的一线影像模态，但梗死对比度微弱，人工勾画病灶既慢又费力。受临床实践中通过比较左右脑半球定位梗死这一做法的启发，作者希望利用脑结构的对称性来开发自动化分割方法，以提高AIS分割的准确性和效率。

Method: 提出的方法包含两个阶段：第一阶段进行头部倾斜校正，将每个扫描对齐到真实解剖学中矢状面；第二阶段应用新颖的AsymFeX（Asymmetric Feature Extraction）模块，该模块通过跨半球注意力、特征差异估计以及双尺度门控，在局部3×3×3邻域内将每个体素与其真实的对侧对应体素进行比较，从而同时捕获大、小梗死病灶。整个方法设计为nnU-Net兼容的两阶段3D分割框架。

Result: 在AISD数据集上，方法达到0.6796 Dice、23.53 mm HD95和7.69 mL AVD，显著优于现有最先进方法。在70 mL溶栓资格阈值上进行了临床相关的容积分析。在ATLAS v2.1和ISLES'24上的概念验证表明，该对称性驱动的设计无需架构更改即可跨成像模态和卒中时间点泛化，并辅以不确定性分析评估临床部署下的可靠性。

Conclusion: 基于脑半球对称性比较的AsymFeX设计在NCCT上显著提升了AIS病灶分割精度，并在多模态、多时间点数据集上展现出良好的泛化能力。该方法在临床溶栓决策阈值（70 mL）下提供了相关的容积分析支持，具备临床部署潜力。代码已开源。

Abstract: Fast and accurate segmentation of Acute Ischemic Stroke (AIS) lesions is essential for stroke prognosis and treatment planning. Non-contrast CT (NCCT), the first-line imaging modality for diagnosing ischemic infarcts, exhibits subtle infarct contrast, making manual delineation slow and labor-intensive. Motivated by this, and by the clinical practice of comparing brain hemispheres to localize infarcts, we propose a two-stage, nnU-Net-compatible 3D segmentation method. The first stage corrects head tilt to align each scan to its true anatomical mid-sagittal plane; the second applies a novel Asymmetric Feature Extraction (AsymFeX) module, comparing each voxel to its true contralateral counterpart within a local 3 x 3 x 3 neighborhood via cross-hemispheric attention, feature disparity estimation, and dual-scale gating to capture both large and small infarcts. On AISD, our method achieves 0.6796 Dice, 23.53 mm HD95, and 7.69 mL AVD, significantly outperforming existing state-of-the-art methods, with clinically relevant volumetric analysis at the 70 mL thrombolysis-eligibility threshold. Proof-of-concept evaluation on ATLAS v2.1 and ISLES'24 demonstrates that the same symmetry-driven design generalizes across imaging modalities and stroke time points without architectural changes, further supported by an uncertainty analysis assessing reliability under clinical deployment. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/biomedia-lab/AIS-detection.

</details>


### [160] [MOSAIC: Modality-agnostic Spectral Alignment for Federated Image-level Weakly Supervised Tumor Segmentation under Client-specific Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19788)
*Tarun Kumar Garg,Vaanathi Sundaresan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出MOSAIC，首个面向客户端缺失模态场景下的模态无关联邦弱监督肿瘤分割框架，通过客户端特定模态对齐模块、光谱原型对齐损失和联邦精炼网络，在仅使用图像级标签的情况下接近全监督精度（FeTS2022上Dice达0.84）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床多中心场景下，各机构持有不完整且异构的模态子集，同时隐私约束禁止集中式数据共享。现有联邦学习虽缓解了数据共享问题，但面临客户端特定缺失模态导致的融合质量与分割性能下降；同时弱监督与联邦学习的联合研究在异构缺失模态+图像级标签场景下仍是空白。

Method: 提出MOSAIC框架，包含三个核心组件：（1）客户端特定模态对齐模块：无需模态身份先验即可将可用通道融合到共享潜在空间；（2）光谱原型对齐损失：利用紧凑的不可逆频域统计量缓解跨客户端分布偏移；（3）联邦精炼网络：对CAM伪标签去噪，生成精确分割掩码，打破弱监督精度上限。

Result: 在三个多机构脑肿瘤基准（FeTS2022、BraTS-MEN、BraTS-SSA）上显著优于所有图像、框和点监督基线；仅用图像级标签即接近全监督精度，在FeTS2022上达到0.84 Dice；支持动态新客户端加入，无需重训练即可在已有联邦中实现0.01-0.04 Dice的性能。

Conclusion: MOSAIC验证了在客户端特定缺失模态和隐私约束下，仅用图像级标签即可实现接近全监督的弱监督联邦分割性能，并支持新机构的动态扩展，为临床多中心可信多模态融合提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Trustworthy multimodal fusion in clinical settings requires handling incomplete and heterogeneous modality subsets across institutions, where privacy constraints prohibit centralized data sharing. Federated learning (FL) mitigates data-sharing constraints but suffers from client-specific missing modalities, where institutions possess incomplete multimodal subsets, degrading fusion quality and segmentation performance. While FL and weak supervision have been studied separately, their joint use with image-level labels under heterogeneous missing modalities remains unaddressed. We propose \textbf{MOSAIC}, the first modality-agnostic federated framework for weakly supervised binary tumor segmentation under client-specific missing modalities. We introduce a client-specific modality-alignment module that fuses available channels into a shared latent space without prior knowledge of modality identity, a spectral prototype alignment loss that reconciles cross-client distribution shift using compact non-invertible frequency-domain statistics, and a dedicated federated refinement network that denoises the resulting CAM pseudo-labels into accurate masks, breaking the accuracy ceiling of weak supervision. Experiments on three multi-institutional brain tumor benchmarks (FeTS2022, BraTS-MEN, and BraTS-SSA) demonstrate significant improvements over all image, box, and point-supervised baselines, approaching fully supervised accuracy using only image-level labels and reaching 0.84 Dice on FeTS2022. Dynamic new client addition enables previously unseen institutions to join an already-trained federation within 0.01-0.04 Dice without retraining. Code is available at https://github.com/Tarun2201/MOSAIC.

</details>


### [161] [Energy-Mamba: A Physics-Constrained State-Space Model for Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19813)
*Mohamed Mabrok,Yalda Zafari,Essam A. Rashed*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出Energy-Mamba，一种将状态空间模型（SSM/Mamba）与物理启发的约束机制相结合的新型架构。通过引入可学习的势能函数和基于梯度（自动微分）的强迫项，将状态演化约束在保持局部图像保真度的低能构型中，模拟哈密顿动力学。在四个医学影像数据集上实现了最先进的分类性能，且参数显著更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba等状态空间模型虽具有线性时间复杂度和长程依赖建模能力，但在将序列模型适配到2D图像时，无约束的状态演化会导致表征漂移——动态隐藏状态逐渐丢失对局部图像特征的保真度。这一问题在医学影像领域尤为关键，因为细粒度局部细节直接决定诊断准确性。

Method: 提出Energy-Mamba Block，在SSM动力学中集成物理启发的约束：通过可学习的势能函数量化演化状态与静态局部特征之间的兼容性；利用自动微分动态计算基于梯度的强迫项，将状态拉向低能量构型以维持局部视觉保真度。整体框架类比哈密顿动力学，其中SSM扫描代表动能，约束函数代表势能，二者共同支配状态轨迹。

Result: 在四个医学影像数据集（视网膜OCT、胸部X射线、显微镜、腹部CT）上，Energy-Mamba取得最先进的分类性能，同时参数数量显著少于现有方法，验证了物理启发的接地约束能同时提升效率和表征质量。

Conclusion: 通过将物理原理（哈密顿动力学）引入SSM架构，Energy-Mamba证明在医学视觉任务中，物理约束能够作为架构先验，使模型学习隐式约束以获取鲁棒且保真的表征，从而在参数效率与性能之间取得更优平衡。

Abstract: State-Space Models (SSMs), particularly Mamba, offer linear-time complexity for long-range dependencies, making them attractive for medical imaging with limited annotated data. However, adapting these sequential models to 2D images through unconstrained state evolution causes representational drift, the dynamic hidden state progressively loses fidelity to local image features. We introduce Energy-Mamba, integrating SSM dynamics with physics-informed constraints via a learnable potential energy function that quantifies compatibility between evolving states and static local features. Our Energy-Mamba Block introduces a gradient-based forcing term, computed dynamically via automatic differentiation, that pulls states toward low-energy configurations maintaining local visual fidelity. This formulation mirrors Hamiltonian dynamics: kinetic energy (SSM scan) plus potential energy (our constraint function) govern state trajectories. This architectural prior enables learning implicit constraints for robust, faithful representations, crucial in medical imaging where fine-grained local detail drives accurate diagnosis. Evaluated on four datasets (retinal OCT, chest X-ray, microscopy, abdominal CT), Energy-Mamba achieves state-of-the-art classification performance with significantly fewer parameters, demonstrating that physics-informed grounding can enhance both efficiency and representational quality in medical vision tasks.

</details>


### [162] [Simulation-to-Real First-Break Segmentation for Efficient Inversion in Musculoskeletal Ultrasound Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19828)
*Yifei Sun,Yubing Li,Yannick Benezeth,Stéphanie Bricq,Yunrong Zhang,Lekang Jiang,Chang Su,Ligang Cui,Weijun Lin*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种学习辅助的超声CT（USCT）重建流程，结合基于分割的初至波提取与混合全波形反演（HFWI），通过轻量级2D U-Net利用初至轨迹的空间连续性进行提取，并采用模拟-真实联合训练策略缓解标注不足和domain gap问题，在体外、离体和在体数据集上验证了稳定性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全波形反演（FWI）在定量肌肉骨骼超声CT中具有前景，但骨相关的散射、衰减和信号退化使其对初始声学属性分布高度敏感且易发生周期跳跃（cycle skipping）。传统逐道STA/LTA初至拾取在弱信号、空间异质或系统噪声干扰下不可靠，亟需更稳健的初至提取方法以构建高质量初始模型。

Method: 提出一条学习辅助重建流水线：1) 轻量级2D U-Net将初至波穿越接收通道的轨迹作为分割目标，利用空间连续性而非逐道独立处理；2) 采用任务特定模拟数据（增强真实系统噪声）预训练，逐步增加信号退化的staged训练，并以弱标注实验数据仅对解码器进行微调；3) 将提取的初至与Rytov近似的走时信息结合，在早期反演阶段将走时信息与波形拟合融合到HFWI中。

Result: 在体外phantom、离体牛肢和活体人腿数据集上，相比传统STA/LTA拾取，该方法提供更空间连贯的初至轨迹、更低的平均提取误差，且数秒内处理完整全矩阵采集数据集。集成到HFWI后，提取的初至改善了初始模型构建，在局部初至SNR低于3 dB的挑战性场景下仍能稳定驱动后续FWI重建。

Conclusion: 基于分割的初至提取结合HFWI的学习辅助重建流水线，有效克服了骨骼USCT中的弱初至和低SNR挑战，为定量肌肉骨骼超声CT提供了一种稳健且高效的完整解决方案。

Abstract: Full-waveform inversion (FWI) is a promising strategy for quantitative musculoskeletal ultrasound computed tomography (USCT), but bone-related scattering, attenuation, and signal degradation make it highly sensitive to the accuracy of the initial acoustic-property distributions and prone to cycle skipping. First-arrival traveltimes provide important kinematic information for initial-model construction, yet conventional trace-wise picking is unreliable when arrivals are weak, spatially heterogeneous, or buried in system noise. We propose a learning-assisted reconstruction pipeline that combines segmentation-based first-arrival extraction with hybrid full-waveform inversion (HFWI), which incorporates Rytov-approximation-based traveltime information together with waveform fitting during the early inversion stage. A lightweight 2D U-Net treats the first-arrival trajectory across receiver channels as a first-break segmentation target and exploits its spatial continuity rather than processing each trace independently. To address both limited manual annotations and the simulation-to-real gap, the network is pretrained on task-specific simulations augmented with real system-noise recordings, followed by stage-wise training with progressively increased signal degradation and decoder-only fine-tuning using limited weakly labeled experimental data. The method is evaluated on in vitro phantom, ex vivo bovine-limb, and in vivo human-thigh datasets. Compared with conventional STA/LTA picking, the proposed network yields more spatially coherent first-arrival trajectories, lower mean extraction errors, and processes a full-matrix-capture dataset within seconds. When integrated into HFWI, the extracted arrivals improve initial-model construction and lead to stable subsequent FWI reconstructions, including challenging cases with estimated local first-arrival SNRs below 3 dB.

</details>


### [163] [Flow Matching-Based PET Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20112)
*Fumio Hashimoto,Ziqian Huang,Tatsuya Yokota,Kuang Gong*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于流匹配（flow matching）的PET图像重建方法，以克服扩散模型需要大量反向采样步骤和数据一致性更新的问题。作者首先建立了PET-FlowDPS方法，将泊松似然引导和基于EM的预条件器融入FlowDPS框架；随后提出一种基于模型的PET重建方法，将预训练的流匹配模型作为先验，并在近似贝叶斯框架中解释流先验、PET数据细化和随机传播。在[18F]FDG脑PET数据集上的实验表明，该方法在不同剂量水平下相比参考方法取得了更好的偏差-方差权衡，展示了流匹配作为定量PET图像重建生成先验的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型用于PET重建时需要大量反向采样步骤，且需将数据一致性更新嵌入采样过程，计算效率低。流匹配方法可以从中间状态直接估计干净图像，使数据一致性细化与流传播分离，因此有望提高PET重建的效率和性能。

Method: 1）建立PET-FlowDPS方法：将泊松似然引导与基于EM的预条件器整合进FlowDPS框架；2）提出基于模型的PET重建方法：以预训练的流匹配模型作为生成先验，在近似贝叶斯框架中联合解释流先验、PET数据细化和随机传播过程。

Result: 在[18F]FDG脑PET数据集上，提出的方法在不同剂量水平下较其他参考方法获得更好的偏差-方差权衡表现，证明了方法的有效性和优势。

Conclusion: 流匹配可作为定量PET图像重建的有效生成先验，在保证重建质量的同时提升效率，为PET重建提供了新的可行方案。

Abstract: Generative models have shown strong potential for positron emission tomography (PET) image reconstruction. Although diffusion model-based reconstruction methods have demonstrated promising performance, they often require many reverse sampling steps with data-consistency updates incorporated into the sampling process. Flow matching offers an attractive alternative because it can directly estimate clean images from intermediate states, allowing data-consistency refinement to be separated from flow propagation. In this work, we proposed flow matching-based PET image reconstruction methods. We first established PET-FlowDPS by incorporating Poisson likelihood guidance with an expectation-maximization (EM)-based preconditioner into the FlowDPS framework. We then proposed a model-based PET reconstruction method that used a pretrained flow matching model as a prior, in which the flow-based prior, PET data refinement, and stochastic propagation were interpreted within an approximate Bayesian framework. Experimental results using [$^{\text{18}}\text{F}$]FDG brain PET datasets showed that the proposed method achieved better bias-variance trade-offs across different dose levels compared with other reference methods. These results demonstrated the potential of flow matching as a generative prior for quantitative PET image reconstruction.

</details>
